.fold-head{background:#eaf2ed;color:#365849;font-weight:700;vertical-align:middle}
.fold-head span{display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;max-width:105px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.fold-toggle{width:22px;height:22px;line-height:18px;padding:0;margin-right:5px;border:1px solid #b8cbbb;border-radius:5px;background:#fff;color:#24553f;font-weight:800;font-size:16px;vertical-align:middle}
.fold-minus{margin:0 4px 2px 0;width:18px;height:18px;line-height:14px;font-size:14px}
.fold-cell{background:#f8faf8;color:#829087;text-align:center;font-size:10px;vertical-align:middle}</style><style>
table{table-layout:fixed;width:max-content;min-width:2200px;font-size:11px}
th,td{box-sizing:border-box;min-width:0;max-width:none;padding:6px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-word;white-space:normal}
th{height:auto;line-height:1.25;font-size:10px;vertical-align:top}
.thtext{white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.3;min-height:2.5em;max-width:100%}
th:first-child,td:first-child{position:sticky;left:0;z-index:6;width:150px;background:#f2f6f3}
td:first-child{background:#fff;z-index:3}
th:nth-child(2),td:nth-child(2){position:sticky;left:150px;z-index:5;width:42px;background:#f2f6f3}
td:nth-child(2){background:#fff;z-index:2}
td .cell{box-sizing:border-box;width:100%;min-width:0;max-width:100%;display:block;padding:5px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:6px;background:#fff;font-size:10px;white-space:normal}
td select.cell{appearance:auto}
td .img{width:58px;height:58px;object-fit:contain}
td.status{min-width:0}
.status div{display:block;white-space:normal;line-height:1.35;padding:1px 0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.precheck{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;min-width:0}
.pre-main{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;align-items:stretch}
.pre-field{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;min-width:0;color:#62736a;font-size:9px;line-height:1.2}.pre-field .cell{font-size:11px}.pre-note{grid-column:1/-1}.bulk-pre{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 8px}.bulk-pre label{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;font-size:11px;color:#52665a}.bulk-pre select,.bulk-pre input{border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 7px;background:#fff;min-height:30px}.bulk-pre select{min-width:72px}.translation-status{font-size:10px;color:#687a71;max-width:280px}.pager{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px;padding:8px 4px;color:#607269;font-size:11px}.pager button,.pager select{border:1px solid #dbe5df;background:#fff;border-radius:7px;padding:5px 8px}.pager button:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}.save-state{font-size:10px;color:#52745f;white-space:nowrap}
td a{display:inline-block;max-width:100%;overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-all}
.fbtn.active{background:#cde9d8;color:#08775e}
.group-toggle{display:block;margin-top:5px;padding:4px 5px;border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:6px;background:#edf6ef;color:#285844;font-size:9px;white-space:normal;max-width:100%}
.filter-options{max-height:280px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid #e5ece7;border-radius:7px;padding:5px}
.filter-options label{display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:flex-start;padding:4px 2px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:11px}
.filter-options label[hidden]{display:none}
.status-filter-group{border:1px solid #e5ece7;border-radius:7px;padding:7px;margin:7px 0}
.status-filter-group summary{font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:5px}
.status-search{box-sizing:border-box;width:100%;padding:6px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:5px;margin:4px 0}
.status-filter-values{max-height:135px;overflow:auto}
.status-filter-values label{display:flex;gap:6px;padding:4px 2px;font-size:11px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.status-filter-values label[hidden]{display:none}
.toolbar{flex-wrap:wrap}
.toolbar input[type=search]{flex:1 1 240px;min-width:180px}
.gridbox{max-width:100%;overflow:auto}
@media(max-width:850px){.page{padding:14px 9px}.toolbar>*{flex:1 1 auto}.stats{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}}
.control-shell{position:sticky;top:66px;z-index:12;background:#f4f7f5;padding:6px 0 8px}
.toolbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:9px;background:#fff;border:1px solid #e0e8e3;border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:0;box-shadow:0 2px 8px #173b2410}
.toolbar input[type=search]{flex:1 1 300px;min-width:230px;border:1px solid #e3eae5;background:#f7f9f7;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;outline-color:#7bb6a2}
.logic-note{font-size:10px;color:#718078;white-space:nowrap}
.filter-summary{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:7px 3px 2px;color:#55675e;font-size:11px}
.filter-summary b{font-size:10px;color:#5d7467;font-weight:600}
.filter-chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;padding:4px 2px 1px;max-height:66px;overflow:auto}
.filter-chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;max-width:360px;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #cfe2d5;border-radius:999px;background:#edf7f0;color:#315a45;font-size:10px}
.filter-chip span{overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.filter-chip button{border:0;background:none;color:#536e60;font-weight:800;padding:0 1px}
.page{max-width:none;width:100%;padding:16px 20px}
.gridbox{max-height:calc(100vh - 290px)}
@media(max-width:850px){.control-shell{top:58px}.toolbar>*{flex:1 1 auto}.logic-note{white-space:normal}.gridbox{max-height:calc(100vh - 320px)}}<style>
.toolbar-secondary{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:7px 0 2px;border-top:1px solid #edf2ee}
.grouping-controls,.history-controls{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:11px;color:#496357}
.grouping-controls input,.grouping-controls select,.history-controls select{box-sizing:border-box;min-height:30px;border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:7px;background:#fff;padding:5px 7px;color:#29463a}
.grouping-controls input{width:66px}.grouping-controls select{min-width:105px}.history-controls select{max-width:210px;min-width:150px}
.filterpop{box-sizing:border-box;overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-gutter:stable}
.filter-actions{position:sticky;bottom:-11px;z-index:2;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:9px 0 3px;margin:8px -2px -2px;background:linear-gradient(#fff,#fff);border-top:1px solid #e4ece7}
.status-value-chip{display:inline-block;max-width:100%;padding:2px 7px;margin:2px 3px 2px 0;border-radius:10px;background:#edf5ef;color:#315c47;font-size:10px;line-height:1.4;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.modelid-line{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:4px;font-weight:650;overflow-wrap:normal;white-space:nowrap}.modelid-line>span{white-space:nowrap;word-break:normal;overflow-wrap:normal;flex:0 0 auto}.share-btn{flex:0 0 auto;width:23px;height:23px;border:1px solid #cfe0d5;border-radius:7px;background:#f5faf6;color:#167653;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;line-height:1}.share-btn:hover{background:#e5f3e9}
.product-card{box-sizing:border-box;overflow:auto;padding:14px;border:1px solid #d5e2da;border-radius:13px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 12px 40px #102c1d35;color:#233b30;font-size:12px}.product-card-close{position:sticky;float:right;top:0;border:0;border-radius:50%;width:26px;height:26px;background:#eef4ef;color:#385747;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer}.product-card-images{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin:2px 0 10px}.product-card-images figure{margin:0;min-width:0}.product-card-images figcaption{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#607269;margin:0 0 4px}.product-card-images img,.product-card-empty{box-sizing:border-box;width:100%;height:clamp(82px,20vh,132px);object-fit:contain;background:#f5f8f6;border-radius:8px}.product-card-empty{display:grid;place-items:center;color:#84948b}.product-card-title{font-size:15px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.45}.product-card-model{margin-top:4px;color:#63756b;line-height:1.4}.product-card-block{margin-top:10px;padding-top:9px;border-top:1px solid #e8eee9}.product-card-label{display:block;margin-bottom:3px;font-size:10px;color:#76867d}.product-card-value{overflow-wrap:anywhere;line-height:1.45}.product-card dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:90px 1fr;gap:7px;margin:12px 0 2px}.product-card dt{color:#76867d}.product-card dd{margin:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.product-card a{color:#167653}.product-card-actions{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:12px}.product-card-actions button{border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:7px;background:#f4f8f5;padding:7px 9px;color:#285844;font-weight:650}
.filterpop{position:fixed}.filterpop .filter-close{position:sticky;float:right;top:0;z-index:10;width:27px;height:27px;margin:-4px -3px 0 6px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;color:#40584c;font-size:17px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer}.close-dialog[hidden]{display:none}.close-dialog{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:200;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:16px;background:#16291f66}.close-dialog-card{max-width:430px;padding:22px;border-radius:14px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 16px 50px #00190f40}.close-dialog-card h3{margin:0 0 8px}.close-dialog-card p{color:#63756b;line-height:1.55}.close-dialog-card>div{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px;margin-top:18px}
@media(max-width:720px){.toolbar-secondary{align-items:flex-start}.history-controls select{max-width:145px;min-width:110px}.product-card{max-width:calc(100vw - 16px)}}
.stats{margin-top:12px}.filterpop .filter-actions{position:sticky;bottom:-11px;z-index:5;background:#fff;padding-bottom:9px;box-shadow:0 -5px 12px #fff}.group-body tr.group-parent-sticky td{position:sticky;top:var(--table-head-height,0px);z-index:3;background:#edf5ef;box-shadow:0 2px 5px #173b241c}.group-body tr.group-parent-sticky td:first-child{z-index:7;background:#edf5ef}.group-body tr.group-parent-sticky td:nth-child(2){z-index:6;background:#edf5ef}.version-modal{width:min(560px,96vw);max-height:calc(100vh - 24px);display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden}.version-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px}.version-list{overflow:auto;max-height:60vh;margin:12px 0;border:1px solid #e4ebe6;border-radius:8px}.version-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:24px minmax(0,1fr);gap:8px;align-items:start;padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid #edf1ee}.version-row:last-child{border-bottom:0}.version-row small{display:block;color:#738178;margin-top:3px}.version-actions{position:sticky;bottom:0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:10px 0 0;background:#fff;border-top:1px solid #e7eee9}.version-actions>div{display:flex;gap:7px}
.gridbox{position:relative;isolation:isolate;overflow:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain}#table thead th{position:sticky!important;top:0!important;z-index:40!important;background:#f2f6f3!important;background-clip:padding-box}#table thead th:first-child{z-index:44!important}#table thead th:nth-child(2){z-index:43!important}.product-card{padding:11px}.product-card-images{margin-bottom:6px}.product-card-block{margin-top:6px;padding-top:6px}.product-card dl{gap:5px;margin:8px 0 0}.product-card-actions{margin-top:8px}@media(max-height:620px){.product-card{padding:8px}.product-card-images img,.product-card-empty{height:72px}.product-card-images figcaption{font-size:9px;margin-bottom:2px}.product-card-block{margin-top:4px;padding-top:4px}.product-card-label{margin-bottom:1px}.product-card dl{gap:3px;margin-top:5px}.product-card-actions{margin-top:5px}}.fold-head{text-align:center!important;vertical-align:middle!important}.fold-head .fold-toggle{display:block;margin:0 auto 5px}.fold-head span{display:block;margin:0 auto;text-align:center;max-width:100%}.group-body tr.group-child td{background:#fff8dc!important}.group-body tr.group-child:hover td{background:#fff1b8!important}.note-editor{height:136px;min-height:136px;resize:vertical;white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.35}.image-zoom{box-sizing:border-box;padding:12px;border:1px solid #d6e2da;border-radius:12px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 14px 45px #102c1d42;pointer-events:auto}.image-zoom img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#f6f8f6;border-radius:8px}.translation-editor{min-height:78px;height:78px;resize:vertical;white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.35}buyer-choice{min-height:36px}.share-image{position:relative}.share-badges{position:absolute;right:5px;top:5px;z-index:2;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-end;gap:4px;max-width:calc(100% - 10px);pointer-events:none}.share-badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;max-width:100%;padding:3px 7px;border:1px solid #ffffffc9;border-radius:999px;background:#fff9e7ee;color:#68480a;box-shadow:0 2px 8px #14291b24;font-size:10px;line-height:1.2;font-weight:750;white-space:nowrap}.share-badge i{font-style:normal;font-size:12px}.share-badge.crown{background:#fff4d3;color:#80550a}.share-link-modal{width:min(680px,96vw)}.share-link-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}.share-link-head h3{margin:0}.share-link-controls{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0}.share-link-controls[hidden]{display:none}.share-link-controls input,.share-inline-link{flex:1 1 300px;min-width:0;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:7px;padding:8px}.share-link-controls button,.product-card-actions button{border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:7px;background:#f4f8f5;padding:7px 9px;color:#285844;font-weight:650}.product-card-actions{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap}.product-card-actions .share-inline-link{min-width:0;width:100%;flex:1 1 100%}.modalbg[hidden]{display:none!important}</style><body><header class="top"><div class="brand"><div class="logo">寻</div><div>寻品工作看板 <span class="muted">· Buyer Pool Eyeball</span></div></div><div class="actions"><input hidden id="file" type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xlsm,.csv,.txt"><input hidden id="translationFile" type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xlsm,.csv,.txt"><input hidden id="invitationFile" type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xlsm,.csv,.txt"><datalist id="buyerDevOptions"></datalist><datalist id="buyerReasonOptions"></datalist><button class="btn" id="import">导入 Excel / CSV</button><span class="translation-status" id="translationState">使用参考 HTML 的词库与队列翻译，按当前页逐步补齐中文</span><button class="btn primary" id="export">导出 Excel</button><button class="btn" id="exportCsv">导出 CSV</button></div></header>
<main class="page"><section class="title"><div><span class="muted">源表字段结构 · 买手筛选与核价</span><h1>寻品工作看板</h1><p>严格按源表标题、顺序和第二行字段说明展示；无筛选时显示全部导入记录。</p></div><span class="tag" id="state">等待导入商品数据</span></section>
<div class="control-shell"><div class="toolbar"><input type="search" id="search" placeholder="跨字段搜索 Model ID、标题、类目、价格等"><button class="btn" id="allFilters">所有字段筛选</button><button class="btn" id="clear">清空筛选</button><button class="btn" id="schemaBtn">字段来源</button><select id="sort"><option value="desc">目标价：高到低</option><option value="asc">目标价：低到高</option></select><span class="logic-note">列条件跨字段按“且”、同列多选按“或”；全文搜索匹配任一字段</span></div><div class="toolbar-secondary"><span class="grouping-controls"><b>同组归类相似度</b><select id="thresholdPreset" aria-label="相似度预设"><option value="10">10% 宽松</option><option value="30">30%</option><option value="50">50%</option><option value="70">70%</option><option value="90" selected>90% 严格</option><option value="custom">自定义</option></select><input id="thresholdInput" type="number" min="1" max="100" value="90" aria-label="自定义相似度百分比">%<button class="btn" id="rebuildGroups">按阈值分组</button><button class="btn" id="clearGroups">取消分组</button><span id="groupState" class="muted">导入默认平铺；选择相似度后手动分组</span><button class="btn" id="onlineTranslate" title="按参考 HTML 翻译源代码，为当前页商品补齐中文">翻译当前页中文</button><button class="btn" id="importTranslations">导入中文翻译</button><button class="btn" id="importInvitations">导入邀请ID</button></span><span class="history-controls"><b>历史版本</b><select id="versionSelect" aria-label="选择历史版本"><option value="">暂无历史版本</option></select><button class="btn" id="openVersion">查看</button><button class="btn" id="restoreVersion">恢复为当前</button><button class="btn" id="returnCurrent" hidden>返回当前</button><button class="btn" id="saveVersion">保存版本</button><button class="btn" id="manageVersions">删除历史版本</button><button class="btn" id="saveClose">关闭工作台</button></span></div><div class="filter-summary"><span id="result"></span><span id="saveState" class="save-state">本机自动保存</span><b id="filterBadge">未添加筛选条件</b></div><div id="filterChips" class="filter-chips" aria-live="polite"></div></div>
<div id="bulk" class="bulk" hidden><b id="selText"></b><span class="bulk-pre"><label>是否放前置核价 <select id="bulkPreValue" aria-label="批量设置是否放前置核价"><option value="">不修改</option><option value="Y">Y</option><option value="N">N</option></select></label><label>核价日期 <input id="bulkPreDate" type="date" aria-label="批量设置核价日期"></label><button class="btn primary" id="bulkPreApply">应用到所选</button></span><button class="btn" id="bulkEdit">其他字段批量填充</button> <button class="btn" id="merge">合并选中组</button> <button class="btn" id="splitGroup">移出同组，单独展示</button> <button class="btn" id="unmerge">取消手动合并</button> <button class="btn" id="shareSelected">生成所选在线分享链接</button> <button class="btn" id="unselect">清除勾选</button></div>
<div class="gridbox"><table id="table"></table></div><div id="pager" class="pager"></div><div class="stats"><div class="stat"><span>筛选后商品组</span><b id="groupsN">0</b></div><div class="stat"><span>筛选后 Model ID</span><b id="modelsN">0</b></div><div class="stat"><span>待买手判断</span><b id="pendingN">0</b></div><div class="stat"><span>无 1688 前置核价</span><b id="priceN">0</b></div></div><p class="help">每页显示 100 个商品组；筛选和导出仍按全部数据计算。买手编辑会自动保存到本机数据库；请始终用同一浏览器和本地启动器打开。</p></main><div id="closeDialog" class="close-dialog" hidden><section class="close-dialog-card"><h3>关闭工作台前保存</h3><p>工作台会自动保存到本机。确认保存后，可关闭当前浏览器标签页；下次使用同一浏览器和本地启动器会恢复数据。</p><div><button class="btn" id="cancelClose">继续工作</button><button class="btn primary" id="confirmSaveClose">确认保存</button></div></section></div><div id="overlay"></div><div id="versionManager"></div><div id="shareLinkDialog" class="modalbg" hidden></div><div id="toast"></div>
<script>
(function(){
const H=["是否可开发（添加筛选器）","不可开发原因（添加筛选器）","level1_global_be_category（添加筛选器）","level2_global_be_category（添加筛选器）","level3_global_be_category（添加筛选器）","model状态（添加筛选器）","主图图片","model 图片","Shopee link","item name\n原文（添加筛选器）","item name\n中文（添加筛选器）","Model name\n原文（添加筛选器）","Model name\n中文（添加筛选器）","Non Brand 倒算价","1688链接","1688价格","目标价","C8倒算价\n（默认折叠，点击+号可展开查看）","走C8目标价\n（默认折叠，点击+号可展开查看）","价格check","品牌（默认折叠，点击+号可展开查看）","款式（默认折叠，点击+号可展开查看）","件数（默认折叠，点击+号可展开查看）","是否放前置核价","model备注","邀请ID"];
const S=["是\n否\n待定-倒算价可做，待1688前置核价\n待定-倒算价可做，1688价格申诉中\n待定-其他\n\n(这个部分买手可以自主选择以上几个选项，默认空白)","26/已开发\n27/已重复\n28/禁售类目\n29/禁运类目\n30/超重限制\n31/IP侵权\n32/敏感词(宗教/政治敏感产品/虚拟/盲盒/定制产品等)\n33/赠品/补差\n34/季节性品-节庆品过季\n35/品牌品-大品牌暂不可开发(无quota)\n36/品牌品-Local品牌且无法在中国source到相似品牌(无quota)\n37/原品链接失效\n38/原品model信息不全无法开发\n39/全网找不到相似款，source难度大\n40/类目错配(备注EL和非EL)\n41/其他原因(需备注)\n41/其他原因-虚标\n41/其他原因-CB品牌暂未入驻/无法提品((且无quota))\n41/其他原因-倒算价小于0.5\n41/其他原园-倒算价>=0.5仍不可开发\n41/其他原因-其他价格原因\n41/其他原因-需要强售前售后支持\n41/其他原因-长尾类目但整个item只有单model\n41/其他原因-高rr，不适用全托管\n\n(这个部分买手可以自主选择以上几个选项，默认空白)","系统抓取level1_global_be_category","系统抓取level2_global_be_category","系统抓取level3_global_be_category","系统抓取下面这几个字段的结果展示\npool_type\nis_top_1k\ncspu_group_type_for_testing\ngrass_region\nasp_tag_by_l1\nsource_type_new\nsourcing_status_daily_refresh\n\n(这个部分集中展示，能买手清晰知道这个品的状态，6个结果)","系统抓取item_image表头字段","系统抓取model_image表头字段，如果值为空默认系统抓取item_image表头字段","系统抓取product_link字段","系统抓取item_name","参考 HTML 翻译源代码：优先词库，未命中后按队列在线翻译；可手动编辑","系统抓取model_name","参考 HTML 翻译源代码：优先词库，未命中后按队列在线翻译；可手动编辑","系统抓取backcal_cogs_non_brand","系统抓取final_1688_link","系统抓取final_1688_price_moq_3","系统自己算，取倒算价和1688价格最低值，min（Non Brand 倒算价&1688价格）","系统抓取backcal_cogs_white_label","系统自己算，取倒算价和1688价格最低值，min（C8倒算价&1688价格）","系统抓取backcal_cogs_non_brand是否≥final_1688_price_moq_3","优先抓取 mall_brand_name；为空时从 item name 原文和 Model name 原文提取\n买手可以手动修改结果","系统抓取item_name和model name归纳\n如白色\n\n这个地方默认展示系统结果，但是结果方框买手可自己修改结果，自定义","系统抓取item_name和model name归纳\n3pcs\n\n这个地方默认展示系统结果，但是结果方框买手可自己修改结果，自定义","当final_1688_price_moq_3为空值的时候，显示“未前置核价”\n买手可以选择是否放核价：Y/N\n前置核价日期：可双击选择当前年月日\n备注：买手可以自行备注","买手自行备注","买手上传 Model ID 与邀请ID后自动匹配；可编辑" ];
const FIELD=H.map((h,i)=>({h:h,s:S[i],i:i,display:h.replace(/[（(][^）)]*[）)]/g,"").trim(),filter:true,foldGroup:i>=17&&i<=18?"c8":i>=20&&i<=22?"attrs":""}));

let rows=[],headers=H.slice(),sourceFileName="",selected=new Set(),filters={},opened=new Set(),groupView=true,showFold=false,showC8=false,showAttrs=false,threshold=90,groupingEnabled=false,historyMode=false,liveSnapshot=null,historyRows=[],hasUnsavedChanges=false,saveWaiters=[];let pageIndex=0,pageSize=100,visibleRows=[],activeHashRun=0,dbPromise=null,saveTimer=null,searchTimer=0,saveAllPending=false,dirtyRows=new Set(),saveInProgress=false,saveFailureNotified=false,activeProductCard="",activeZoomSource="",imageZoomTimer=0;const statusFilterKeys=["pool_type","is_top_1k","cspu_group_type_for_testing","grass_region","asp_tag_by_l1","source_type_new","sourcing_status_daily_refresh"];
let buyerCustomOptions={};try{buyerCustomOptions=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pool-buyer-custom-options-v1")||"{}")}catch(e){};
const rawLookupCache=new WeakMap(),rawSchemaLookup=new Map();const $=x=>document.getElementById(x),esc=x=>String(x==null?"":x).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c])),norm=x=>String(x||"").toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_（）():：-]/g,""),number=x=>{if(x==null||String(x).trim()==="")return null;let n=Number(String(x).replace(/[¥,$，\s]/g,""));return Number.isFinite(n)?n:null},toast=x=>{$("toast").textContent=x;setTimeout(()=>$("toast").textContent="",2600)};
const keyMap=[["是否可开发","dev"],["不可开发原因","reason"],["level1_global_be_category","l1"],["level2_global_be_category","l2"],["level3_global_be_category","l3"],["model状态","status"],["主图图片","main"],["model图片","modelimage"],["shopee link","product"],["item name原文","itemoriginal"],["item name中文","itemchinese"],["model name原文","modeloriginal"],["model name中文","modelchinese"],["non brand倒算价","nb"],["1688链接","ali"],["1688价格","price"],["目标价","target"],["c8倒算价","c8"],["走c8目标价","c8target"],["价格check","check"],["品牌","brand"],["款式","style"],["件数","qty"],["是否放前置核价","pre"],["model备注","note"],["邀请ID","invite"]];
function key(h){let n=norm(h);if(!n)return "";let mapped=keyMap.find(x=>n.includes(norm(x[0]))||norm(x[0]).includes(n));if(mapped)return mapped[1];let aliases=[["item_image","main"],["main_image","main"],["model_image","modelimage"],["product_link","product"],["item_name","itemoriginal"],["model_name","modeloriginal"],["backcal_cogs_non_brand","nb"],["final_1688_link","ali"],["final_1688_price_moq_3","price"],["backcal_cogs_white_label","c8"]];return aliases.find(x=>norm(x[0])===n)?.[1]||""}
function safeHttpUrl(value){let text=String(value||"").trim();if(!text)return "";try{let u=new URL(text,location.href);return u.protocol==="https:"||u.protocol==="http:"?u.href:""}catch(e){return ""}}
function safeImageUrl(value){let text=String(value||"").trim();if(/^data:image\/(?:png|jpe?g|webp|gif);base64,/i.test(text))return text;return safeHttpUrl(text)}
function headerScore(row){let known=new Set(H.concat(FIELD.map(f=>f.display),statusFilterKeys,["item_image","main_image","model_image","product_link","item_name","model_name","model_id","backcal_cogs_non_brand","final_1688_link","final_1688_price_moq_3","backcal_cogs_white_label","invitation_id","invite_id","Invitation ID"]).map(norm));return row.reduce((n,h)=>n+(known.has(norm(h))?1:0),0)}
function isMetaRow(values){let s=values.map(x=>String(x??"")).join(" ");return /系统抓取|自主选择以上几个选项|默认折叠，点击\+号|这个部分买手可以自主选择/.test(s)}
function getRaw(r,aliases){let raw=r?.raw||{},cache=rawLookupCache.get(raw);if(!cache){let keys=Object.keys(raw),signature=keys.join("\u001f");cache=rawSchemaLookup.get(signature);if(!cache){let exact=new Map(),entries=[];for(let h of keys){let n=norm(h);entries.push([n,h]);if(!exact.has(n))exact.set(n,h)}cache={exact,entries};rawSchemaLookup.set(signature,cache)}rawLookupCache.set(raw,cache)}for(let alias of aliases){let h=cache.exact.get(norm(alias));if(h!=null&&raw[h]!==""&&raw[h]!=null)return raw[h]}for(let alias of aliases){let n=norm(alias);for(let [key,h] of cache.entries)if(key.includes(n)&&raw[h]!==""&&raw[h]!=null)return raw[h]}return ""}
function invalidateRaw(r){if(r?.raw)rawLookupCache.delete(r.raw)}
function normalizeModelId(value){let id=String(value??"").replace(/^\uFEFF/,"").trim().replace(/[\s,]/g,"");if(!id)return "";if(/^\d+\.0+$/.test(id))id=id.replace(/\.0+$/,"");if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?e[+-]?\d+$/i.test(id)){let n=Number(id);if(Number.isSafeInteger(n))id=String(n)}return id}function idFor(r){let id=normalizeModelId(getRaw(r,["Model ID","model_id","modelid","模型id"]));return id||"AUTO-"+String(r.excelRow||0).padStart(5,"0")}
function round2(n){let x=Number(n);return Math.round((x+Math.sign(x||1)*Number.EPSILON)*100)/100}
function format2(n){let x=number(n);return x==null?"":round2(x).toFixed(2)}
function calc(r,k){let p=number(getRaw(r,["1688价格","final_1688_price_moq_3"]));if(k==="target"){let n=number(getRaw(r,["Non Brand 倒算价","backcal_cogs_non_brand"])),v=n!=null&&p!=null?Math.min(n,p):n??p??number(getRaw(r,["目标价"]));return v==null?"":format2(v)}if(k==="c8target"){let c=number(getRaw(r,["C8倒算价","backcal_cogs_white_label"])),v=c!=null&&p!=null?Math.min(c,p):c??p??number(getRaw(r,["走C8目标价"]));return v==null?"":format2(v)}if(k==="check"){let n=number(getRaw(r,["Non Brand 倒算价","backcal_cogs_non_brand"]));return n==null||p==null?"":n>=p?"是":"否"}return null}
function value(r,h){if(norm(h).includes("modelid"))return idFor(r);if(norm(h).includes("商品组"))return r.manualGroup||r.group;if(h==="前置核价日期")return r.preDate||"";if(h==="前置核价备注")return r.preNote||"";let k=key(h);if(k==="target"||k==="c8target"||k==="check")return calc(r,k);if(k==="nb")return format2(getRaw(r,["Non Brand 倒算价","backcal_cogs_non_brand"]));if(k==="c8")return format2(getRaw(r,["C8倒算价","backcal_cogs_white_label"]));if(k==="main")return r.main||getRaw(r,["主图图片","item_image"]);if(k==="modelimage")return r.modelImage||getRaw(r,["model 图片","model_image"])||r.main||"";if(k==="status")return statusLines(r).map(x=>x[1]).join("\n");if(k==="pre")return r.preValue??getRaw(r,["是否放前置核价"]);if(k==="preDate")return r.preDate||"";if(k==="preNote")return r.preNote||"";let raw=r.raw||{},direct=raw[h],edited=(r.userEditedFields||[]).includes(h);if(edited||Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,h))return direct??"";return getRaw(r,aliasFor(k))??""}
function filterValue(r,h){let k=key(h);if(norm(h).includes("modelid"))return idFor(r);if(k==="main"||k==="modelimage")return String(value(r,h)||"").trim()?"有图片":"空白";return String(value(r,h)??"").trim()}
function aliasFor(k){let x={dev:["是否可开发"],reason:["不可开发原因"],l1:["level1_global_be_category"],l2:["level2_global_be_category"],l3:["level3_global_be_category"],product:["Shopee link","product_link"],itemoriginal:["item name 原文","item_name"],itemchinese:["item name 中文"],modeloriginal:["Model name 原文","model_name"],modelchinese:["Model name 中文"],nb:["Non Brand 倒算价","backcal_cogs_non_brand"],ali:["1688链接","final_1688_link"],price:["1688价格","final_1688_price_moq_3"],c8:["C8倒算价","backcal_cogs_white_label"],brand:["品牌","mall_brand_name"],style:["款式"],qty:["件数"],note:["model备注"],invite:["邀请ID","invitation_id","invite_id"]};return x[k]||[]}
function drawOptions(i) {
  let options = [];
  if (i === 0) options = ["是","否","待定-倒算价可做，待1688前置核价","待定-倒算价可做，1688价格申诉中","待定-其他"];
  if (i === 1) options = ["26/已开发","27/已重复","28/禁售类目","29/禁运类目","30/超重限制","31/IP侵权","32/敏感词(宗教/政治敏感产品/虚拟/盲盒/定制产品等)","33/赠品/补差","34/季节性品-节庆品过季","35/品牌品-大品牌暂不可开发(无quota)","36/品牌品-Local品牌且无法在中国source到相似品牌(无quota)","37/原品链接失效","38/原品model信息不全无法开发","39/全网找不到相似款，source难度大","40/类目错配(备注EL和非EL)","41/其他原因(需备注)","41/其他原因-虚标","41/其他原因-CB品牌暂未入驻/无法提品((且无quota))","41/其他原因-倒算价小于0.5","41/其他原因-倒算价>=0.5仍不可开发","41/其他原因-其他价格原因","41/其他原因-需要强售前售后支持","41/其他原因-长尾类目但整个item只有单model","41/其他原因-高rr，不适用全托管"];
  const field = H[i], custom = field ? buyerCustomOptions[norm(field)] || [] : [];
  return Array.from(new Set(options.concat(custom)));
}
function buyerOptionListId(field) { return field === H[0] ? "buyerDevOptions" : "buyerReasonOptions"; }
function refreshBuyerDatalists() {
  const d = $("buyerDevOptions"), r = $("buyerReasonOptions");
  if (d) d.innerHTML = drawOptions(0).map(x => "<option value=\"" + esc(x) + "\"></option>").join("");
  if (r) r.innerHTML = drawOptions(1).map(x => "<option value=\"" + esc(x) + "\"></option>").join("");
}
function rememberBuyerOption(field, value) {
  const v = String(value || "").trim(), i = H.indexOf(field);
  if (!v || (i !== 0 && i !== 1)) return;
  const base = drawOptions(i).filter(x => !(buyerCustomOptions[norm(field)] || []).includes(x));
  if (base.includes(v)) return;
  const list = buyerCustomOptions[norm(field)] || [];
  if (!list.includes(v)) {
    list.push(v); buyerCustomOptions[norm(field)] = list;
    try { localStorage.setItem("pool-buyer-custom-options-v1", JSON.stringify(buyerCustomOptions)); } catch (e) {}
    refreshBuyerDatalists();
  }
}

function statusValue(r,k){let v=getRaw(r,[k]);if(String(v??"").trim()!=="")return String(v).trim();let direct=String(getRaw(r,["model状态"])||"").trim();if(!direct)return "";try{let parsed=JSON.parse(direct);if(parsed&&typeof parsed==="object"){for(let key in parsed)if(norm(key)===norm(k))return String(parsed[key]??"").trim()}}catch(e){}let re=new RegExp("(?:^|[\\r\\n;,|])\\s*"+k+"\\s*[:：=]\\s*([^\\r\\n;,|]*)","i"),m=direct.match(re);if(m)return m[1].trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g,"");let lines=direct.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean),at=lines.findIndex(x=>norm(x.replace(/[:：=].*$/,""))===norm(k));if(at>=0){let same=lines[at].match(/[:：=]\s*(.*)$/);return (same?same[1]:lines[at+1]||"").trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g,"")}let ix=statusFilterKeys.indexOf(k);if(lines.length===statusFilterKeys.length&&ix>=0)return lines[ix].replace(/^.*?[:：=]\s*/,"").trim();if(lines.length===statusFilterKeys.length*2&&ix>=0)return lines[ix*2+1].replace(/^.*?[:：=]\s*/,"").trim();return ""}
function statusLines(r){return statusFilterKeys.map(k=>[k,statusValue(r,k)]).filter(x=>x[1]!=="")}
function groupIsOpen(g){return g==="c8"?showC8:showAttrs}
function visibleFields(){return FIELD.filter(f=>f.foldGroup!==""&&groupIsOpen(f.foldGroup)||!f.foldGroup)}
function displayColumns(){let out=[],foldAdded=new Set(),openSeen=new Set();for(let f of FIELD){if(f.foldGroup&&!groupIsOpen(f.foldGroup)){if(!foldAdded.has(f.foldGroup)){foldAdded.add(f.foldGroup);out.push({h:f.foldGroup==="c8"?"C8/核价列":"品牌/款式/件数",display:f.foldGroup==="c8"?"C8/核价（2列）":"品牌/款式/件数（3列）",s:"点击加号展开此列组。",k:"fold",foldGroup:f.foldGroup})}continue}let x=Object.assign({},f,{k:key(f.h)});if(f.foldGroup&&!openSeen.has(f.foldGroup)){x.collapseGroup=f.foldGroup;openSeen.add(f.foldGroup)}out.push(x)}return out}

function colSize(f){let w={id:150,select:36,fold:120,dev:82,reason:145,l1:80,l2:80,l3:80,status:170,main:72,modelimage:72,product:88,itemoriginal:112,itemchinese:112,modeloriginal:112,modelchinese:112,nb:80,ali:96,price:72,target:72,c8:76,c8target:82,check:68,brand:72,style:76,qty:48,pre:180,note:220,invite:112};return w[f.k]||118}
function updateStats(a,g){$("groupsN").textContent=g.length;$("modelsN").textContent=a.length;$("pendingN").textContent=a.filter(r=>!String(getRaw(r,["是否可开发"])||"").trim()).length;$("priceN").textContent=a.filter(r=>!number(getRaw(r,["1688价格","final_1688_price_moq_3"]))).length;$("state").textContent=rows.length?(sourceFileName?sourceFileName+" · ":"")+rows.length+" 条源表记录":sourceFileName?sourceFileName+" · 未发现商品数据行":"等待导入商品数据";$("bulk").hidden=!selected.size;$("selText").textContent="已选 "+selected.size+" 个 Model ID"}
function matchesState(state,v){if(!state)return true;let hasValues=Array.isArray(state.values),hasQuery=!!state.query,valueMatch=hasValues&&state.values.includes(String(v??"").trim()),queryMatch=hasQuery&&norm(v).includes(norm(state.query));if(hasValues&&hasQuery)return valueMatch&&queryMatch;if(hasValues)return valueMatch;return hasQuery?queryMatch:true}
function updateSelectionUI(){let selectedVisible=0;for(let r of visibleRows)if(selected.has(r.uid))selectedVisible++;let all=$("selectVisible");if(all){all.checked=visibleRows.length>0&&selectedVisible===visibleRows.length;all.indeterminate=selectedVisible>0&&selectedVisible<visibleRows.length}$("bulk").hidden=!selected.size;$("selText").textContent="已选 "+selected.size+" 个 Model ID";document.querySelectorAll("[data-select]").forEach(x=>x.checked=selected.has(x.dataset.select))}function filtered(){let q=norm($("search").value),idState=filters["Model ID"],fieldStates=FIELD.filter(f=>filters[f.h]),stateKeys=statusFilterKeys.filter(k=>filters["status:"+k]);if(!q&&!idState&&!fieldStates.length&&!stateKeys.length)return rows.slice();return rows.filter(r=>{if(q&&!norm(FIELD.map(f=>value(r,f.h)).join(" ")+" "+idFor(r)).includes(q))return false;if(idState&&!matchesState(idState,idFor(r)))return false;for(let f of fieldStates)if(!matchesState(filters[f.h],filterValue(r,f.h)))return false;for(let k of stateKeys)if(!matchesState(filters["status:"+k],statusValue(r,k)))return false;return true})}
function selectedSummary(values){return values.length?values.slice(0,3).map(v=>v||"（空白）").join(" 或 ")+(values.length>3?" 等 "+values.length+" 项":""):"无匹配项"}
function activeFilterItems(){let a=[],q=$("search").value.trim();if(q)a.push({id:"__search",label:"全文搜索："+q});let idState=filters["Model ID"];if(idState){let bits=[];if(idState.values)bits.push(selectedSummary(idState.values));if(idState.query)bits.push("包含 "+idState.query);if(bits.length)a.push({id:"Model ID",label:"Model ID："+bits.join(" 且 ")})}for(let f of FIELD){let s=filters[f.h];if(!s)continue;let bits=[];if(s.values)bits.push(selectedSummary(s.values));if(s.query)bits.push("包含 "+s.query);if(bits.length)a.push({id:f.h,label:f.display+"："+bits.join(" 且 ")})}for(let k of statusFilterKeys){let s=filters["status:"+k];if(!s)continue;let bits=[];if(s.values)bits.push(selectedSummary(s.values));if(s.query)bits.push("包含 "+s.query);if(bits.length)a.push({id:"status:"+k,label:k+"："+bits.join(" 且 ")})}return a}
function renderFilterSummary(a,g){$("result").textContent="当前结果："+a.length+" 个 Model ID · "+g.length+" 个商品组";let items=activeFilterItems();$("filterBadge").textContent=items.length?items.length+" 个筛选条件":"未添加筛选条件";$("filterChips").innerHTML=items.map(x=>'<span class="filter-chip" title="'+esc(x.label)+'"><span>'+esc(x.label)+'</span><button type="button" aria-label="移除筛选条件" data-remove-filter="'+esc(x.id)+'">×</button></span>').join("");document.querySelectorAll("[data-remove-filter]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(b.dataset.removeFilter==="__search")$("search").value="";else delete filters[b.dataset.removeFilter];render()})}
function sorted(list){let dir=$("sort")?.value==="asc"?1:-1;return list.slice().sort((a,b)=>{let x=number(calc(a,"target")),y=number(calc(b,"target"));if(x==null)return y==null?(a.excelRow||0)-(b.excelRow||0):1;if(y==null)return -1;return (x-y)*dir})}
function recordsGroups(list){let m=new Map();for(let r of list){let id=r.manualGroup||r.group||r.uid;if(!m.has(id))m.set(id,[]);m.get(id).push(r)}let groups=[...m.entries()];for(let [,a] of groups)a.sort((x,y)=>(number(calc(y,"target"))??-Infinity)-(number(calc(x,"target"))??-Infinity));let dir=$("sort")?.value==="asc"?1:-1;groups.sort((a,b)=>{let x=number(calc(a[1][0],"target")),y=number(calc(b[1][0],"target"));if(x==null)return y==null?0:1;if(y==null)return -1;return (x-y)*dir});return groups}
function renderPager(totalGroups,totalModels){let pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(totalGroups/pageSize)),first=totalGroups?pageIndex*pageSize+1:0,last=Math.min(totalGroups,(pageIndex+1)*pageSize);$("pager").innerHTML='<span>显示组 '+first+'–'+last+' / '+totalGroups+'（'+totalModels+' 个 Model ID）</span><button type="button" id="pagePrev" '+(pageIndex<=0?'disabled':'')+'>上一页</button><span>第 '+(pageIndex+1)+' / '+pages+' 页</span><button type="button" id="pageNext" '+(pageIndex>=pages-1?'disabled':'')+'>下一页</button><label>每页 <select id="pageSize">'+[50,100,200,500].map(n=>'<option value="'+n+'" '+(n===pageSize?'selected':'')+'>'+n+'</option>').join('')+'</select> 组</label>';$("pagePrev").onclick=()=>{if(pageIndex>0){pageIndex--;render()}};$("pageNext").onclick=()=>{if(pageIndex<pages-1){pageIndex++;render()}};$("pageSize").onchange=()=>{pageSize=Number($("pageSize").value)||100;pageIndex=0;render()}}
function render(){closeProductCard();closeImageZoom();let a=filtered(),g=recordsGroups(a),idcol={h:"Model ID",display:"Model ID",s:"源表没有 Model ID 时按源表行号自动生成。",k:"id",filter:true},selcol={h:"勾选",display:"勾选",s:"选择 Model ID 以进行批量填充或手动合并。",k:"select",filter:false},cols=[idcol,selcol].concat(displayColumns()),collapsed=FIELD.length-visibleFields().length,pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(g.length/pageSize));pageIndex=Math.max(0,Math.min(pageIndex,pages-1));let start=pageIndex*pageSize,pageGroups=g.slice(start,start+pageSize);visibleRows=[];for(let [gid,list] of pageGroups){visibleRows.push(list[0]);if(opened.has(gid))for(let r of list.slice(1))visibleRows.push(r)}updateStats(a,g);renderFilterSummary(a,g);$("result").textContent="当前结果："+a.length+" 个 Model ID · "+g.length+" 个商品组；26 个数据表头，折叠 "+collapsed+" 列";renderPager(g.length,a.length);let html="<colgroup>"+cols.map(f=>"<col style=\"width:"+colSize(f)+"px\">").join("")+"</colgroup><thead><tr>"+cols.map(f=>{if(f.k==="select")return "<th title=\"勾选本页可见 Model ID\"><input id=\"selectVisible\" type=\"checkbox\" aria-label=\"勾选本页可见 Model ID\"></th>";if(f.k==="fold")return "<th class=\"fold-head\"><button type=\"button\" class=\"fold-toggle\" data-fold=\""+f.foldGroup+"\" title=\"展开列组\">＋</button><span>"+esc(f.display)+"</span></th>";let active=filters[f.h]||(f.k==="id"&&filters["Model ID"])||(f.k==="status"&&statusFilterKeys.some(k=>filters["status:"+k]));return "<th title=\""+esc(f.s)+"\"><div class=\"thtext\">"+(f.collapseGroup?'<button type=\"button\" class=\"fold-toggle fold-minus\" data-fold=\"'+f.collapseGroup+'\" title=\"折叠列组\">−</button>':"")+esc(f.display).replace(/\n/g,"<br>")+"</div>"+(f.filter?"<button class=\"fbtn "+(active?"active":"")+"\" data-filter=\""+esc(f.h)+"\">"+(f.k==="status"?"7项状态筛选 ▾":"筛选 ▾")+"</button>":"")+"</th>"}).join("")+"</tr></thead>";if(!rows.length)html+="<tbody><tr><td colspan=\""+cols.length+"\" class=\"empty\"><b>等待导入商品数据</b>所选文件如果只有表头和字段说明，会显示 0 条记录；请选择包含商品数据行的 XLSX 或 CSV。</td></tr></tbody>";else if(!a.length)html+="<tbody><tr><td colspan=\""+cols.length+"\" class=\"empty\">当前筛选没有匹配记录</td></tr></tbody>";else for(let [gid,list] of pageGroups){html+='<tbody class="group-body">'+rowHtml(list[0],cols,list.length,gid,false);if(opened.has(gid))for(let r of list.slice(1))html+=rowHtml(r,cols,list.length,gid,true);html+="</tbody>"}$("table").innerHTML=html;let grid=document.querySelector(".gridbox"),thead=$("table").querySelector("thead");if(grid&&thead)grid.style.setProperty("--table-head-height",thead.getBoundingClientRect().height+"px");updateSelectionUI();bindTable();queueVisibleTranslation()}
function rowHtml(r, cols, count, gid, isChild) {
  const disabled = historyMode ? " disabled" : "";
  const rowClass = !isChild && count > 1 && opened.has(gid) ? "group-parent-sticky" : isChild ? "group-child" : "";
  return "<tr class=\"" + rowClass + "\">" + cols.map(f => {
    if (f.k === "id") {
      const label = opened.has(gid) ? "收起同组型号" : "同组型号 · " + count + " 个";
      return "<td><div class=\"modelid-line\"><span>" + esc(idFor(r)) + "</span><button class=\"share-btn\" type=\"button\" data-share=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" title=\"生成产品信息卡片\" aria-label=\"预览 " + esc(idFor(r)) + "\">↗</button></div>" + (count > 1 && !isChild ? "<button class=\"group-toggle\" data-toggle=\"" + esc(gid) + "\"" + disabled + ">" + label + "</button>" : isChild ? "<span class=\"muted\">同组展开明细</span>" : "") + "</td>";
    }
    if (f.k === "select") return "<td><input class=\"selectbox\" type=\"checkbox\" data-select=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" " + (selected.has(r.uid) ? "checked" : "") + disabled + "></td>";
    if (f.k === "fold") return "<td class=\"fold-cell\">该组已折叠</td>";
    const k = f.k, v = value(r, f.h);
    if (k === "main" || k === "modelimage") {
      const imageUrl = safeImageUrl(v);
      return "<td>" + (imageUrl ? "<img class=\"img\" loading=\"lazy\" data-zoom=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" src=\"" + esc(imageUrl) + "\" alt=\"" + esc(f.display) + "\">" : k === "main" ? "<input type=\"file\" accept=\"image/*\" data-image=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" data-kind=\"main\"" + disabled + ">" : "—") + "</td>";
    }
    if (k === "status") {
      const lines = statusLines(r);
      return "<td class=\"status\">" + (lines.length ? lines.map(([field, val]) => "<span class=\"status-value-chip\" title=\"" + esc(field) + "\">" + esc(val) + "</span>").join("") : "—") + "</td>";
    }
    if (k === "pre") {
      return "<td><div class=\"precheck\"><label class=\"pre-field\"><span>是否放前置核价</span><select class=\"cell\" data-edit=\"preValue\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" aria-label=\"是否放前置核价\"" + disabled + "><option value=\"\">请选择</option><option value=\"Y\" " + (r.preValue === "Y" ? "selected" : "") + ">Y</option><option value=\"N\" " + (r.preValue === "N" ? "selected" : "") + ">N</option></select></label><label class=\"pre-field\"><span>核价日期</span><input class=\"cell\" type=\"date\" data-edit=\"preDate\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" value=\"" + esc(r.preDate || "") + "\" aria-label=\"前置核价日期\"" + disabled + "></label><label class=\"pre-field\"><span>买手备注</span><input class=\"cell pre-note\" data-edit=\"preNote\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" value=\"" + esc(r.preNote || "") + "\" placeholder=\"填写前置核价备注\" aria-label=\"前置核价备注\"" + disabled + "></label></div></td>";
    }
    if (k === "dev" || k === "reason") {
      const list = buyerOptionListId(f.h);
      return "<td><input class=\"cell buyer-choice\" list=\"" + list + "\" data-edit=\"raw\" data-field=\"" + esc(f.h) + "\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" value=\"" + esc(v) + "\" placeholder=\"选择默认项或输入自定义\" title=\"可从默认选项中选择，也可直接输入自定义内容；输入过的内容会保留为可选项\"" + disabled + "></td>";
    }
    if (k === "note") return "<td><textarea class=\"cell note-editor\" data-edit=\"raw\" data-field=\"" + esc(f.h) + "\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" placeholder=\"填写 Model 备注\"" + disabled + ">" + esc(v) + "</textarea></td>";
    if (k === "itemchinese" || k === "modelchinese") return "<td><textarea rows=\"4\" class=\"cell translation-editor\" data-edit=\"raw\" data-field=\"" + esc(f.h) + "\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" placeholder=\"中文翻译结果，可编辑\" title=\"中文翻译结果，可编辑；较高文本框便于查看完整内容\"" + disabled + ">" + esc(v) + "</textarea></td>";
    if (["ali", "price", "brand", "style", "qty", "invite"].includes(k)) {
      const type = k === "price" || k === "qty" ? "type=\"number\" step=\"any\"" : "";
      const placeholder = k === "ali" && !String(v || "").trim() ? " placeholder=\"无前置核价\"" : "";
      return "<td><input class=\"cell\" " + type + " data-edit=\"raw\" data-field=\"" + esc(f.h) + "\" data-id=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" value=\"" + esc(v) + "\"" + disabled + placeholder + "></td>";
    }
    if (["target", "c8target", "check"].includes(k)) return "<td data-calc-row=\"" + esc(r.uid) + "\" data-calc-type=\"" + k + "\">" + esc(v || "—") + "</td>";
    if (k === "product") {
      const href = safeHttpUrl(v);
      return href ? "<td><a href=\"" + esc(href) + "\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">" + esc(v) + " ↗</a></td>" : "<td>" + esc(v || "—") + "</td>";
    }
    return "<td title=\"" + esc(v) + "\">" + esc(v == null || v === "" ? "—" : v) + "</td>";
  }).join("") + "</tr>";
}

function updateCalculatedCells(r){for(let el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-calc-row]"))if(el.dataset.calcRow===r.uid)el.textContent=calc(r,el.dataset.calcType)||"—"}
function closeProductCard(){document.getElementById("productCard")?.remove();activeProductCard=""}
function closeImageZoom(){clearTimeout(imageZoomTimer);imageZoomTimer=0;document.getElementById("imageZoom")?.remove();activeZoomSource=""}
function scheduleImageZoomClose(){clearTimeout(imageZoomTimer);imageZoomTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.querySelector("#imageZoom:hover"))closeImageZoom()},220)}
function showImageZoom(img){let src=img.currentSrc||img.src;if(!src)return;if(activeZoomSource===src&&document.getElementById("imageZoom")){closeImageZoom();return}closeImageZoom();activeZoomSource=src;let rect=img.getBoundingClientRect(),width=Math.min(560,innerWidth-24),height=Math.min(520,innerHeight-24),left=rect.right+width+12<=innerWidth?rect.right+12:Math.max(12,rect.left-width-12),top=Math.max(12,Math.min(rect.top,innerHeight-height-12)),box=document.createElement("aside");box.id="imageZoom";box.className="image-zoom";box.setAttribute("role","dialog");box.setAttribute("aria-label",img.alt||"图片放大预览");box.style.cssText="position:fixed;z-index:130;width:"+width+"px;height:"+height+"px;left:"+left+"px;top:"+top+"px";box.innerHTML=`<img src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(img.alt)}">`;box.onpointerenter=()=>clearTimeout(imageZoomTimer);box.onpointerleave=scheduleImageZoomClose;document.body.append(box)}
function productDetail(r) {
  const get = h => { const v = value(r, h); return v == null ? "" : String(v).trim(); };
  const main = safeImageUrl(r.main || getRaw(r, ["主图图片", "item_image"]));
  const model = safeImageUrl(r.modelImage || getRaw(r, ["model 图片", "model_image"]) || r.main || getRaw(r, ["主图图片", "item_image"]));
  return {id:idFor(r), main:main, model:model, itemChinese:get(H[10]), modelChinese:get(H[12]), category:categoryPath(r), invitation:get(H[25]), target:calc(r,"target") || "—", shopee:get("Shopee link"), shopeeUrl:safeHttpUrl(get("Shopee link")), badges:marketingBadges(r)};
}
function productCardHTML(d, index) {
  const img = (src,label) => src ? "<img src=\"" + esc(src) + "\" alt=\"" + esc(label) + "\">" : "<div class=\"empty\">暂无图片</div>";
  const field = (label,val) => "<div class=\"field\"><b>" + esc(label) + "</b><span>" + esc(val || "—") + "</span></div>";
  const badge = badgesMarkup(d.badges);
  const link = d.shopee ? d.shopeeUrl ? "<a href=\"" + esc(d.shopeeUrl) + "\">" + esc(d.shopee) + "</a>" : esc(d.shopee) : "—";
  return "<article class=\"product\"><div class=\"product-head\"><span>产品 " + (index + 1) + "</span><b>Model ID " + esc(d.id) + "</b></div><div class=\"images\"><figure class=\"share-image\">" + img(d.main,"主图图片") + badge + "<figcaption>主图图片</figcaption></figure><figure>" + img(d.model,"model 图片 / SKU图") + "<figcaption>model 图片（SKU图）</figcaption></figure></div>" + field("item name 中文",d.itemChinese) + field("Model name 中文",d.modelChinese) + "<div class=\"two\">" + field("目标价",d.target) + field("上新类目",d.category) + "</div>" + (d.invitation ? field("邀请ID",d.invitation) : "") + "<div class=\"field\"><b>Shopee link</b><span>" + link + "</span></div></article>";
}
function marketingBadges(r) {
  const lines = statusLines(r);
  const highPrice = lines.some(x => /高价品/.test(String(x[1] || "")));
  const source = String(statusValue(r,"source_type_new") || getRaw(r,["source_type_new"]) || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  const badges = [];
  if (highPrice) badges.push({kind:"diamond",icon:"💎",text:"150%投流"});
  if (source === "A") badges.push({kind:"crown",icon:"👑",text:"运营重点主推款"});
  return badges;
}
function badgesMarkup(badges) {
  return "<div class=\"share-badges\">" + (badges || []).map(x => "<span class=\"share-badge " + esc(x.kind) + "\"><i>" + esc(x.icon) + "</i>" + esc(x.text) + "</span>").join("") + "</div>";
}
function compactShareImage(src) {
  if (!String(src || "").startsWith("data:image/")) return Promise.resolve(src || "");
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => {
      try {
        const scale = Math.min(1, 720 / Math.max(img.naturalWidth || 1, img.naturalHeight || 1));
        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale));
        canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale));
        canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        resolve(canvas.toDataURL("image/webp", 0.76));
      } catch (e) { resolve(src); }
    };
    img.onerror = () => resolve(src);
    img.src = src;
  });
}
function base64Url(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 30000) bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + 30000));
  return btoa(bin).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
}
async function buildOnlineShareLink(records) {
  if (!records || !records.length) throw new Error("请至少选择一个 Model ID");
  const host = location.hostname.toLowerCase();
  const privateIpv4 = /^(10\.|192\.168\.|172\.(1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])\.)/.test(host);
  if (location.protocol !== "https:" || host === "localhost" || host.endsWith(".localhost") || host === "127.0.0.1" || host === "::1" || privateIpv4) {
    throw new Error("当前工作台不是公开 HTTPS 网站，无法生成跨设备可访问的在线链接。请先将工作台和 share.html 部署到公开 HTTPS 站点，再生成分享链接。");
  }
  await ensureCategoryTranslations(records);
  const products = [];
  for (const r of records) {
    const d = productDetail(r);
    d.main = await compactShareImage(d.main);
    d.model = await compactShareImage(d.model);
    products.push(d);
  }
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({v:1,products:products}));
  let token;
  if ("CompressionStream" in window) {
    const zipped = new Uint8Array(await new Response(new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream("gzip"))).arrayBuffer());
    token = "z" + base64Url(zipped);
  } else token = "j" + base64Url(bytes);
  if (token.length > 1800000) throw new Error("图片数据过大，在线链接超过浏览器可用长度；请减少分享产品数量或改用外链图片");
  const shareUrl=new URL("share.html",location.href);shareUrl.searchParams.set("v",token);return shareUrl.href;
}
async function copyShareText(text) {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); return true; }
  catch (e) {
    const input = document.createElement("textarea");
    input.value = text; input.style.position = "fixed"; input.style.opacity = "0";
    document.body.append(input); input.select();
    const ok = document.execCommand("copy"); input.remove(); return ok;
  }
}
async function showShareLinkDialog(records) {
  const dialog = $("shareLinkDialog");
  dialog.hidden = false;
  dialog.innerHTML = "<section class=\"modal share-link-modal\"><div class=\"share-link-head\"><h3>在线产品分享链接</h3><button type=\"button\" class=\"btn\" data-share-close aria-label=\"关闭\">×</button></div><p class=\"muted\" data-share-status>正在整理产品信息和中文类目…</p><div class=\"share-link-controls\" hidden><input readonly aria-label=\"在线产品链接\"><button class=\"btn primary\" data-copy-share>复制链接</button><button class=\"btn\" data-open-share>打开链接</button></div><p class=\"muted\">同一链接包含所选的全部产品，分享数据编码在 v 参数中。工作台与分享页需部署在同一公开在线站点，拿到链接的任何人都能查看；本地地址仅本机可用。</p></section>";
  const close = () => { dialog.hidden = true; };
  dialog.querySelector("[data-share-close]").onclick = close;
  dialog.onclick = e => { if (e.target === dialog) close(); };
  try {
    const link = await buildOnlineShareLink(records);
    const status = dialog.querySelector("[data-share-status]"), controls = dialog.querySelector(".share-link-controls"), input = controls.querySelector("input");
    status.textContent = "已生成 " + records.length + " 个产品的分享链接";
    input.value = link; controls.hidden = false;
    controls.querySelector("[data-copy-share]").onclick = async e => {
      const button = e.currentTarget;
      button.textContent = await copyShareText(link) ? "已复制" : "复制失败";
      setTimeout(() => { button.textContent = "复制链接"; }, 1500);
    };
    controls.querySelector("[data-open-share]").onclick = () => window.open(link, "_blank", "noopener");
  } catch (e) {
    dialog.querySelector("[data-share-status]").textContent = "链接生成失败：" + (e && e.message ? e.message : "请稍后重试");
  }
}
function toggleProductCard(uid, anchor) {
  if (activeProductCard === uid) { closeProductCard(); return; }
  closeProductCard();
  const r = rows.find(x => x.uid === uid);
  if (!r) return;
  const d = productDetail(r), card = document.createElement("aside");
  card.id = "productCard"; card.className = "product-card";
  const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect(), width = Math.min(480, innerWidth - 16), height = Math.min(560, innerHeight - 16);
  const left = rect.right + width + 12 < innerWidth ? rect.right + 8 : Math.max(8, rect.left - width - 8);
  const top = Math.max(8, Math.min(rect.top, innerHeight - height - 8));
  card.style.cssText = "position:fixed;z-index:120;width:" + width + "px;max-height:" + height + "px;left:" + left + "px;top:" + top + "px";
  const image = (src,label,badges) => src ? "<figure class=\"share-image\"><img loading=\"lazy\" src=\"" + esc(src) + "\" alt=\"" + esc(label) + "\">" + badgesMarkup(badges) + "<figcaption>" + esc(label) + "</figcaption></figure>" : "<figure><figcaption>" + esc(label) + "</figcaption><div class=\"product-card-empty\">暂无图片</div></figure>";
  const field = (label,val,attr) => "<div class=\"product-card-block\"><span class=\"product-card-label\">" + esc(label) + "</span><div class=\"product-card-value\"" + (attr || "") + ">" + esc(val || "—") + "</div></div>";
  const link = d.shopee ? d.shopeeUrl ? "<a href=\"" + esc(d.shopeeUrl) + "\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">" + esc(d.shopee) + "</a>" : esc(d.shopee) : "—";
  card.innerHTML = "<button class=\"product-card-close\" type=\"button\" aria-label=\"关闭\">×</button><div class=\"product-card-images\">" + image(d.main,"主图图片",d.badges) + image(d.model,"model 图片（SKU图）",[]) + "</div>" + field("item name 中文",d.itemChinese) + field("Model name 中文",d.modelChinese) + field("上新类目",d.category," data-share-category=\"1\"") + (d.invitation ? field("邀请ID",d.invitation) : "") + "<dl><dt>目标价</dt><dd>" + esc(d.target) + "</dd><dt>Model ID</dt><dd>" + esc(d.id) + "</dd><dt>Shopee link</dt><dd>" + link + "</dd></dl><div class=\"product-card-actions\"><button type=\"button\" data-generate-single>生成在线链接</button></div>";
  document.body.append(card);
  card.querySelector(".product-card-close").onclick = closeProductCard;
  card.querySelector("[data-generate-single]").onclick = async e => {
    const actions = e.currentTarget.parentElement;
    actions.innerHTML = "<span class=\"muted\">正在生成在线链接…</span>";
    try {
      const url = await buildOnlineShareLink([r]);
      actions.innerHTML = "<input class=\"share-inline-link\" readonly aria-label=\"在线分享链接\"><button type=\"button\" data-copy-single>复制链接</button>";
      actions.querySelector("input").value = url;
      actions.querySelector("[data-copy-single]").onclick = async b => { b.currentTarget.textContent = await copyShareText(url) ? "已复制" : "复制失败"; };
    } catch (err) { actions.innerHTML = "<span class=\"muted\">" + esc(err && err.message ? err.message : "链接生成失败") + "</span>"; }
  };
  activeProductCard = uid;
  ensureCategoryTranslations([r]).then(() => {
    const target = card.querySelector("[data-share-category]");
    if (target && document.body.contains(card)) target.textContent = categoryPath(r);
  }).catch(() => {});
}

function filterPopup(h,anchor){
  if(key(h)==="status"){statusFilterPopup(anchor);return}
  document.querySelector(".filterpop")?.remove();
  let allValues=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>filterValue(r,h)))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b)),filterId=norm(h).includes("modelid")?"Model ID":h,current=filters[filterId],draft=new Set(current?.values??allValues),rect=anchor.getBoundingClientRect(),p=document.createElement("div");
  p.className="filterpop";
  let width=Math.min(340,innerWidth-12),left=Math.max(6,Math.min(rect.left,innerWidth-width-6)),top=Math.max(6,Math.min(rect.bottom+4,innerHeight-520));
  p.style.cssText="position:fixed;z-index:100;background:white;padding:11px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:9px;width:"+width+"px;max-height:min(520px,calc(100vh - 12px));overflow:auto;left:"+left+"px;top:"+top+"px";
  p.innerHTML=`<button class="filter-close" id="filterClose" type="button" aria-label="关闭筛选">×</button><b>${esc(filterId==="Model ID"?"Model ID":FIELD.find(f=>f.h===h)?.display||h)} · Excel 筛选</b><p class="muted">候选值覆盖全部 Model ID；勾选值按“或”匹配，包含条件与勾选值按“且”组合。候选较多时可搜索。</p><input id="fvSearch" style="width:100%;padding:7px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:6px" placeholder="搜索候选值"><p><input id="fvContains" style="width:100%;padding:7px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:6px" placeholder="包含筛选（可手动输入）" value="${esc(current?.query||"")}"></p><div id="fvCount" class="muted"></div><div class="filter-options" id="fvOptions"></div><div class="filter-actions"><button class="btn" id="fvAll">全选搜索结果</button> <button class="btn" id="fvNone">清空全部勾选</button><button class="btn" id="fvClear">清除此列</button> <button class="btn primary" id="fvApply">应用筛选</button></div>`;
  document.body.append(p);p.querySelector("#filterClose").onclick=()=>p.remove();
  let search=p.querySelector("#fvSearch"),options=p.querySelector("#fvOptions"),matchingValues=allValues;
  function drawOptions(){let q=norm(search.value);matchingValues=allValues.filter(v=>!q||norm(v||"（空白）").includes(q));let displayed=matchingValues.slice(0,250);options.innerHTML=displayed.map(v=>`<label data-filter-option data-text="${esc(v||"（空白）")}"><input type="checkbox" data-fv="${esc(v)}" ${draft.has(v)?"checked":""}> ${esc(v||"（空白）")}</label>`).join("");p.querySelector("#fvCount").textContent=matchingValues.length>250?`匹配 ${matchingValues.length} 项，当前显示前 250 项；全选/清空会作用于全部匹配值`:`共 ${matchingValues.length} 个候选值`;options.querySelectorAll("[data-fv]").forEach(x=>x.onchange=()=>{x.checked?draft.add(x.dataset.fv):draft.delete(x.dataset.fv)})}
  search.oninput=drawOptions;
  p.querySelector("#fvAll").onclick=()=>{for(let v of matchingValues)draft.add(v);drawOptions()};
  p.querySelector("#fvNone").onclick=()=>{draft.clear();drawOptions()};
  p.querySelector("#fvClear").onclick=()=>{delete filters[filterId];p.remove();render()};
  p.querySelector("#fvApply").onclick=()=>{let values=draft.size<allValues.length?[...draft]:null,query=p.querySelector("#fvContains").value.trim();if(values!==null||query)filters[filterId]={...(values!==null?{values}:{}),...(query?{query}:{})};else delete filters[filterId];p.remove();render()};
  drawOptions()
}function statusFilterPopup(anchor){document.querySelector(".filterpop")?.remove();let p=document.createElement("div"),rect=anchor.getBoundingClientRect(),vals={},draft={},parts=[];p.className="filterpop";for(let k of statusFilterKeys){vals[k]=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>statusValue(r,k)))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));let id="status:"+k,state=filters[id];draft[k]=new Set(state?.values||vals[k]);parts.push("<details class=\"status-filter-group\" open><summary>"+esc(k)+(state?" · 已设置":"")+"</summary><input class=\"status-search\" data-status-search=\""+esc(k)+"\" placeholder=\"包含筛选 "+esc(k)+"\" value=\""+esc(state?.query||"")+"\"><div><button class=\"btn\" type=\"button\" data-status-all=\""+esc(k)+"\">全选</button> <button class=\"btn\" type=\"button\" data-status-none=\""+esc(k)+"\">清空勾选</button></div><div class=\"status-filter-values\">"+vals[k].map(v=>"<label data-status-option><input type=\"checkbox\" data-status-key=\""+esc(k)+"\" data-status-value=\""+esc(v)+"\" "+(draft[k].has(v)?"checked":"")+"> "+esc(v||"（空白）")+"</label>").join("")+"</div></details>")}let width=Math.min(420,innerWidth-12),left=Math.max(6,Math.min(rect.left,innerWidth-width-6)),top=Math.max(6,Math.min(rect.bottom+4,innerHeight-520));p.style.cssText="position:fixed;z-index:100;background:white;padding:12px;border:1px solid #dce6df;border-radius:9px;width:"+width+"px;max-height:min(520px,calc(100vh - 12px));overflow:auto;left:"+left+"px;top:"+top+"px";p.innerHTML="<button class=\"filter-close\" id=\"filterClose\" type=\"button\" aria-label=\"关闭筛选\">×</button><b>model状态 · 7 个独立字段</b><p class=\"muted\">字段之间按“且”组合；同一字段勾选多个值按“或”匹配。也可输入包含内容。</p>"+parts.join("")+"<div class=\"filter-actions\"><button class=\"btn\" id=\"statusClear\">清空全部状态条件</button> <button class=\"btn primary\" id=\"statusApply\">应用状态筛选</button></div>";document.body.append(p);p.querySelector("#filterClose").onclick=()=>p.remove();p.querySelectorAll("[data-status-key]").forEach(x=>x.onchange=()=>{let k=x.dataset.statusKey;x.checked?draft[k].add(x.dataset.statusValue):draft[k].delete(x.dataset.statusValue)});p.querySelectorAll("[data-status-all]").forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>{let k=x.dataset.statusAll;draft[k]=new Set(vals[k]);p.querySelectorAll('[data-status-key="'+k+'"]').forEach(y=>y.checked=true)});p.querySelectorAll("[data-status-none]").forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>{let k=x.dataset.statusNone;draft[k]=new Set();p.querySelectorAll('[data-status-key="'+k+'"]').forEach(y=>y.checked=false)});p.querySelector("#statusClear").onclick=()=>{for(let k of statusFilterKeys)delete filters["status:"+k];delete filters[H[5]];p.remove();render()};p.querySelector("#statusApply").onclick=()=>{for(let k of statusFilterKeys){let id="status:"+k,query=p.querySelector('[data-status-search="'+k+'"]').value.trim(),values=draft[k].size<vals[k].length?[...draft[k]]:null;if(values||query)filters[id]={...(values?{values}:{}),...(query?{query}:{})};else delete filters[id]}p.remove();render()}}
function drawer(kind){let content;if(kind==="schema")content=FIELD.map(f=>"<div class=\"schema\"><b>"+esc(f.display)+"</b><div>"+esc(f.s)+"</div></div>").join("")+"<div class=\"schema\"><b>Model ID</b><div>源表未提供此字段时，由工作台按源表行号生成；导入文件若有 Model ID 列则优先读取。</div></div>";else{let entries=[{id:"Model ID",label:"Model ID"}].concat(FIELD.map(f=>({id:f.h,label:f.display})),statusFilterKeys.map(k=>({id:"status:"+k,label:"model状态 · "+k})));content="<p class=\"muted\">输入内容会在对应字段中做包含筛选；不同字段条件按“且”组合。要多选具体值，请点表头的筛选器。</p>"+entries.map(x=>"<div class=\"filterrow\"><label>"+esc(x.label)+"</label><input data-dfilter=\""+esc(x.id)+"\" value=\""+esc(filters[x.id]?.query||"")+"\" placeholder=\"输入包含内容\"></div>").join("")} $("overlay").innerHTML="<div class=\"panel\"><aside class=\"drawer\"><div class=\"dhead\"><b>"+(kind==="schema"?"字段内容与来源（Excel 第 2 行）":"所有字段筛选")+"</b><button class=\"btn\" id=\"closeDrawer\">关闭</button></div>"+content+"</aside></div>";$("closeDrawer").onclick=()=>$("overlay").innerHTML="";$("overlay").querySelector(".panel").onclick=e=>{if(e.target.classList.contains("panel"))$("overlay").innerHTML=""};document.querySelectorAll("[data-dfilter]").forEach(x=>x.oninput=()=>{let id=x.dataset.dfilter,q=x.value.trim(),s=filters[id]||{};if(q)s.query=q;else delete s.query;if(s.values||s.query)filters[id]=s;else delete filters[id];render()})}
function batch() {
  const fields = [H[0],H[1],H[20],H[21],H[22],H[24]];
  const rowsHtml = fields.map(h => {
    const name = FIELD.find(f => f.h === h)?.display || h, choice = h === H[0] || h === H[1];
    const list = choice ? " list=\"" + buyerOptionListId(h) + "\"" : "";
    return "<div class=\"rowf\"><label>" + esc(name) + "</label><input class=\"cellinput\"" + list + " data-batch=\"" + esc(h) + "\" placeholder=\"不修改；默认项可选，也可输入自定义\"></div>";
  }).join("");
  $("overlay").innerHTML = "<div class=\"modalbg\"><div class=\"modal\"><h3>其他字段批量填充</h3><p>作用于 " + selected.size + " 个已选 Model ID；空白字段保持不变。是否可开发和不可开发原因可选默认项，也可输入新值；新值会加入本机选项。</p>" + rowsHtml + "<div class=\"rowf\"><label>1688价格</label><input type=\"number\" step=\"0.01\" data-batch=\"1688价格\"></div><button class=\"btn\" id=\"cancel\">取消</button> <button class=\"btn primary\" id=\"applyBatch\">应用</button></div></div>";
  $("cancel").onclick = () => $("overlay").innerHTML = "";
  $("applyBatch").onclick = () => {
    const vals = {};
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-batch]").forEach(x => vals[x.dataset.batch] = x.value);
    for (const r of rows.filter(x => selected.has(x.uid))) {
      let changed = false;
      for (const k in vals) if (vals[k] !== "") {
        const field = k === "1688价格" ? H[15] : k;
        r.raw[field] = vals[k]; invalidateRaw(r);
        r.userEditedFields = Array.from(new Set([...(r.userEditedFields || []),field]));
        if (field === H[0] || field === H[1]) rememberBuyerOption(field, vals[k]);
        if (field === H[20]) r.autoBrand = false;
        changed = true;
      }
      if (changed) markDirty(r);
    }
    $("overlay").innerHTML = ""; save(); render();
  };
}

function merge(on){let a=rows.filter(r=>selected.has(r.uid));if(on&&a.length<2){toast("请至少勾选两个 Model ID");return}let id="手动组-"+Date.now();for(let r of a){r.manualGroup=on?id:"";r.group=on?id:"UNGROUPED-"+r.uid;markDirty(r)}selected.clear();if(!on&&groupingEnabled)regroup().then(()=>{save();render()});save();render()}function splitSelectedFromGroups(){let chosen=rows.filter(r=>selected.has(r.uid));if(!chosen.length){toast("请先勾选要移出的 Model ID");return}for(let r of chosen){r.manualGroup="单独-"+r.uid;r.group=r.manualGroup;markDirty(r)}selected.clear();save();render();toast("已将 "+chosen.length+" 个 Model ID 从原组移出并单独展示")}function openWorkbenchDB(){if(dbPromise)return dbPromise;dbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!window.indexedDB){reject(Error("浏览器未开放本地数据库"));return}let req=indexedDB.open("buyer-pool-workbench",2);req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{let db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains("records"))db.createObjectStore("records",{keyPath:"uid"});if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains("meta"))db.createObjectStore("meta",{keyPath:"key"});if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains("versions"))db.createObjectStore("versions",{keyPath:"id"});if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains("versionRecords")){let store=db.createObjectStore("versionRecords",{keyPath:["versionId","uid"]});store.createIndex("versionId","versionId",{unique:false})}};req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>{dbPromise=null;reject(req.error||Error("打开本地数据库失败"))}});return dbPromise}
function dbRequest(req){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||Error("本地数据库请求失败"))})}
async function loadWorkbenchState(){let db=await openWorkbenchDB(),tx=db.transaction(["records","meta"],"readonly"),rowsReq=tx.objectStore("records").getAll(),metaReq=tx.objectStore("meta").get("current"),result=await Promise.all([dbRequest(rowsReq),dbRequest(metaReq)]);return{rows:result[0]||[],meta:result[1]||null}}
function localDateKey(d=new Date()){return String(d.getFullYear())+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}
async function listVersions(){let db=await openWorkbenchDB(),tx=db.transaction(["versions"],"readonly");return (await dbRequest(tx.objectStore("versions").getAll())).sort((a,b)=>b.createdAt-a.createdAt)}

backdrop=$("versionManagerBackdrop"),all=$("selectAllVersions"),del=$("deleteSelectedVersions");$("closeVersionManager").onclick=$("cancelVersionDelete").onclick=()=>box.innerHTML="";backdrop.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===backdrop)box.innerHTML=""};function update(){let checks=[...box.querySelectorAll("[data-version-delete]")],n=checks.filter(x=>x.checked).length;del.disabled=!n;del.textContent="删除所选版本（"+n+"）";all.checked=checks.length>0&&n===checks.length;all.indeterminate=n>0&&n<checks.length}box.querySelectorAll("[data-version-delete]").forEach(x=>x.onchange=update);all.onchange=()=>{box.querySelectorAll("[data-version-delete]").forEach(x=>x.checked=all.checked);update()};del.onclick=async()=>{let ids=[...box.querySelectorAll("[data-version-delete]:checked")].map(x=>x.dataset.versionDelete);if(!ids.length)return;if(!confirm("确定永久删除所选 "+ids.length+" 个历史版本及对应快照记录吗？"))return;del.disabled=true;try{let db=await openWorkbenchDB();await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx=db.transaction(["versions","versionRecords"],"readwrite"),versionsStore=tx.objectStore("versions"),recordIndex=tx.objectStore("versionRecords").index("versionId");tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("删除历史版本失败"));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("删除历史版本已中断"));for(let id of ids){versionsStore.delete(id);let req=recordIndex.openCursor(IDBKeyRange.only(id));req.onsuccess=e=>{let cursor=e.target.result;if(cursor){cursor.delete();cursor.continue()}}}});box.innerHTML="";await refreshVersions();toast("已删除 "+ids.length+" 个历史版本及其快照记录")}catch(e){console.error(e);toast("删除历史版本失败："+(e?.message||"本机存储异常"));del.disabled=false}};update()}
async function saveVersion(){if(historyMode){toast("请先返回当前数据再保存版本");return}save(true);clearTimeout(saveTimer);let ok=await flushSave();if(!ok){toast("自动保存失败，未创建版本");return}let versions=await listVersions(),prefix=localDateKey(),seq=versions.filter(v=>v.id.startsWith(prefix+"-V")).length+1,id=prefix+"-V"+seq,label=id,meta={id,label,createdAt:Date.now(),sourceFileName:sourceFileName,threshold:threshold,groupingEnabled:groupingEnabled,headers:headers.slice(),rowCount:rows.length};let db=await openWorkbenchDB();await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx=db.transaction(["versions","versionRecords"],"readwrite");tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("保存历史版本失败"));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("保存历史版本已中断"));tx.objectStore("versions").put(meta);let store=tx.objectStore("versionRecords");for(let r of rows)store.put({versionId:id,uid:r.uid,record:r})});await refreshVersions(id);toast("历史版本已保存："+label)}
async function readVersion(id){if(!id)throw Error("请先选择历史版本");let db=await openWorkbenchDB(),tx=db.transaction(["versions","versionRecords"],"readonly"),metaReq=tx.objectStore("versions").get(id),rowsReq=tx.objectStore("versionRecords").index("versionId").getAll(IDBKeyRange.only(id)),result=await Promise.all([dbRequest(metaReq),dbRequest(rowsReq)]);if(!result[0])throw Error("历史版本不存在或已删除");return{meta:result[0],rows:result[1].map(x=>x.record)}}
async function showVersion(id){if(!historyMode&&(hasUnsavedChanges||saveAllPending||dirtyRows.size||saveInProgress)){clearTimeout(saveTimer);if(!await flushSave())throw Error("当前编辑尚未保存，无法切换历史版本")}let snap=await readVersion(id);if(!liveSnapshot)liveSnapshot={rows:rows,headers:headers,sourceFileName:sourceFileName,threshold:threshold,groupingEnabled:groupingEnabled};rows=snap.rows;headers=snap.meta.headers||H.slice();sourceFileName=snap.meta.sourceFileName||snap.meta.label;threshold=Number(snap.meta.threshold)||90;groupingEnabled=!!snap.meta.groupingEnabled;if(!groupingEnabled)for(let r of rows)r.group="UNGROUPED-"+r.uid;historyMode=true;filters={};selected.clear();opened.clear();pageIndex=0;$("thresholdInput").value=threshold;$("thresholdPreset").value=[10,30,50,70,90].includes(threshold)?String(threshold):"custom";$("returnCurrent").hidden=false;$("groupState").textContent="历史只读 · "+snap.meta.label;render();toast("正在查看历史版本 "+snap.meta.label)}
function returnToCurrent(){if(!liveSnapshot)return;rows=liveSnapshot.rows;headers=liveSnapshot.headers;sourceFileName=liveSnapshot.sourceFileName;threshold=liveSnapshot.threshold;groupingEnabled=!!liveSnapshot.groupingEnabled;liveSnapshot=null;historyMode=false;filters={};selected.clear();opened.clear();pageIndex=0;$("returnCurrent").hidden=true;$("thresholdInput").value=threshold;$("thresholdPreset").value=[10,30,50,70,90].includes(threshold)?String(threshold):"custom";$("groupState").textContent=groupingEnabled?"图片相似度分组已启用":"当前为平铺未分组";render()}
async function restoreVersionAsCurrent(id){if(!id)throw Error("请先选择历史版本");if(!confirm("将历史版本恢复为当前工作数据？当前数据会被替换，但仍可从历史版本列表查看已保存版本。"))return;let snap=await readVersion(id);rows=snap.rows;headers=snap.meta.headers||H.slice();sourceFileName=snap.meta.sourceFileName||snap.meta.label;threshold=Number(snap.meta.threshold)||90;groupingEnabled=!!snap.meta.groupingEnabled;historyMode=false;liveSnapshot=null;filters={};selected.clear();opened.clear();pageIndex=0;$("returnCurrent").hidden=true;$("thresholdInput").value=threshold;$("thresholdPreset").value=[10,30,50,70,90].includes(threshold)?String(threshold):"custom";await regroup();save(true);render();toast("已恢复为当前数据："+snap.meta.label)}
function markDirty(r){if(historyMode||!r?.uid)return;dirtyRows.add(r.uid);hasUnsavedChanges=true;if($("saveState"))$("saveState").textContent="有未保存的更改 · 正在自动保存"}
function save(all=false){if(historyMode)return;if(all)saveAllPending=true;if(!all&&!dirtyRows.size)return;hasUnsavedChanges=true;$("saveState").textContent="保存中…";clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>flushSave(),120)}
async function flushSave(){if(historyMode)return true;if(saveInProgress)return new Promise(resolve=>saveWaiters.push(resolve));if(!saveAllPending&&!dirtyRows.size){hasUnsavedChanges=false;return true}saveInProgress=true;let failed=false,all=saveAllPending,ids=[...dirtyRows];saveAllPending=false;dirtyRows.clear();let wanted=new Set(ids),records=all?rows.slice():rows.filter(r=>wanted.has(r.uid)),succeeded=false;try{let db=await openWorkbenchDB();await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx=db.transaction(["records","meta"],"readwrite"),store=tx.objectStore("records");tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve();tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("保存本地记录失败"));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("本地保存已中断"));if(all)store.clear();for(let r of records)store.put(r);tx.objectStore("meta").put({key:"current",headers:headers,sourceFileName:sourceFileName,threshold:threshold,groupingEnabled:groupingEnabled,savedAt:Date.now()})});succeeded=true;$(("saveState")).textContent="已保存到本机 · "+new Date().toLocaleTimeString();saveFailureNotified=false}catch(e){failed=true;console.error(e);if(all)saveAllPending=true;else for(let id of ids)dirtyRows.add(id);$("saveState").textContent="本机保存失败 · 请检查存储空间";if(!saveFailureNotified){saveFailureNotified=true;toast("本机保存失败，请先导出 Excel 备份再继续编辑")}}finally{saveInProgress=false;if(!failed&&(saveAllPending||dirtyRows.size)){clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>flushSave(),180)}else{hasUnsavedChanges=failed;if(saveWaiters.length){let waiters=saveWaiters.splice(0);for(let resolve of waiters)resolve(succeeded)}}}return succeeded}
function base64(a){let s="";for(let i=0;i<a.length;i+=30000)s+=String.fromCharCode(...a.subarray(i,i+30000));return btoa(s)}
const hashCache=new Map();

async function hash(src){let key=String(src||"");if(!key)return "";if(hashCache.has(key))return hashCache.get(key);let promise=new Promise(ok=>{let done=false,finish=x=>{if(!done){done=true;ok(x)}},im=new Image(),timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(""),5000);im.crossOrigin="anonymous";im.onload=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);try{let c=document.createElement("canvas"),x=c.getContext("2d",{willReadFrequently:true});function makeHash(draw){c.width=9;c.height=8;x.fillStyle="#fff";x.fillRect(0,0,9,8);draw(x);let d=x.getImageData(0,0,9,8).data,b="";for(let y=0;y<8;y++)for(let q=0;q<8;q++){let a=(y*9+q)*4;b+=d[a]+d[a+1]+d[a+2]>d[a+4]+d[a+5]+d[a+6]?"1":"0"}return b}let side=Math.min(im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight)*.78,cx=(im.naturalWidth-side)/2,cy=(im.naturalHeight-side)/2,center=makeHash(ctx=>ctx.drawImage(im,cx,cy,side,side,0,0,9,8)),scale=Math.min(9/im.naturalWidth,8/im.naturalHeight),w=im.naturalWidth*scale,h=im.naturalHeight*scale,full=makeHash(ctx=>ctx.drawImage(im,(9-w)/2,(8-h)/2,w,h));finish(center+"|"+full)}catch(e){finish("")}};im.onerror=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);finish("")};im.src=key});hashCache.set(key,promise);return promise}
function textTokens(r){let raw=[getRaw(r,["item name原文","item_name","item_name_original"]),getRaw(r,["item name中文","item name中文"]),getRaw(r,["Model name原文","model_name"]),getRaw(r,["Model name中文","Model name中文"]),getRaw(r,["品牌"]),getRaw(r,["款式"]),getRaw(r,["件数"])].join(" ").toLowerCase().replace(/https?:\/\/\S+/g," ").replace(/\b(?:new|hot|sale|free|shipping|official|original|best|selling|promotion|discount|202[0-9])\b/g," ").replace(/\b(?:black|white|red|blue|green|pink|yellow|gray|grey|purple|orange|brown|beige|gold|silver)\b/g," ").replace(/\b\d+\s*(?:pcs?|pieces?|pack|sets?)\b/g," ");let out=new Set();for(let w of raw.match(/[a-z0-9]+/g)||[])if(w.length>1)out.add(w);for(let chunk of raw.match(/[\u4e00-\u9fff]+/g)||[]){if(chunk.length===1)out.add(chunk);else for(let i=0;i<chunk.length-1;i++)out.add(chunk.slice(i,i+2))}return out}
function titleSimilarity(a,b){let x=textTokens(a),y=textTokens(b);if(!x.size||!y.size)return 0;let n=0;for(let t of x)if(y.has(t))n++;return 2*n/(x.size+y.size)}
function imageDistance(a,b){if(!a||!b)return 99;let aa=String(a).split("|"),bb=String(b).split("|");if(aa.length<2||bb.length<2)return 99;let dist=(x,y)=>{if(x.length!==y.length)return 99;let d=0;for(let i=0;i<x.length;i++)if(x[i]!==y[i])d++;return d};return dist(aa[0],bb[0])*.8+dist(aa[1],bb[1])*.2}
function imageKey(r){let src=String(r.main||r.modelImage||"");if(!src)return "";if(src.startsWith("data:"))return src;try{let u=new URL(src,location.href);return (u.host+u.pathname).toLowerCase().replace(/@resize[^/]*$/,"").replace(/\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/,"")}catch(e){return src.split(/[?#]/)[0].toLowerCase()}}
function sameImage(a,b){let x=imageKey(a),y=imageKey(b);return !!x&&x===y}
function imageSimilarity(a,b){if(sameImage(a,b))return 100;if(!a?.hash||!b?.hash)return null;let d=imageDistance(a.hash,b.hash),length=Math.max(String(a.hash).split("|")[0].length,String(b.hash).split("|")[0].length,1);return Math.max(0,100*(1-2*d/length))}
function similarityPercent(a,b){let image=imageSimilarity(a,b);if(image==null)return titleSimilarity(a,b)*100;let title=titleSimilarity(a,b)*100;return image*.85+title*.15}
function similarProduct(a,b){let t=Math.max(1,Math.min(100,Number(threshold)||90)),image=imageSimilarity(a,b),title=titleSimilarity(a,b)*100;if(image==null)return t<=50&&title>=t;return image>=Math.min(99,t-5)&&similarityPercent(a,b)>=t}
function escapeRe(s){return s.replace(/[.*+?^$()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&")}
