function translateTitle(text){let s=String(text||"").trim();if(!s)return s;let cached=translationCache.get(s);return cached||s;}
function extractBrand(text){let brands=["Oral-B iO","Philips Sonicare","SmartSonic+","Oral-B","Oral B","Philips","Xiaomi","Mijia","Panasonic","Samsung","Apple","Sony","Bosch","Deerma","Dreame","Baseus","Anker","JBL","Remax","Kemei","VGR","Tefal","Dyson","Braun","Colgate","Oclean","Usmile","KIWIBIRD","Freegrin","Toshiba","Canon","Nikon","Huawei","Lenovo","Logitech","Ugreen","ZMI","Oraimo","Soundcore","Haylou","QCY","Midea","Haier","TCL","Hisense","Roborock","Ecovacs","Beko","Amazfit","Xiaomi Redmi"];for(let b of brands){let re=new RegExp("(?:^|[^a-z0-9])"+escapeRe(b)+"(?:$|[^a-z0-9])","i");if(re.test(text))return b}return ""}
function extractStyle(text){let a=[];for(let [re,label] of [[/\bblack\b|preto|negro|đen|สีดำ|黑色/i,"黑色"],[/\bwhite\b|branco|branca|blanco|blanca|trắng|สีขาว|白色/i,"白色"],[/\bred\b|vermelho|vermelha|rojo|roja|đỏ|สีแดง|红色/i,"红色"],[/\bblue\b|azul|xanh dương|น้ำเงิน|蓝色/i,"蓝色"],[/\bgreen\b|verde|xanh lá|สีเขียว|绿色/i,"绿色"],[/\bpink\b|rosa|rosado|rosada|hồng|สีชมพู|粉色/i,"粉色"],[/\byellow\b|amarelo|amarela|amarillo|amarilla|vàng|สีเหลือง|黄色/i,"黄色"],[/\bgray\b|\bgrey\b|cinza|gris|xám|สีเทา|灰色/i,"灰色"],[/\bpurple\b|roxo|roxa|morado|morada|ม่วง|紫色/i,"紫色"],[/\borange\b|laranja|naranja|cam|ส้ม|橙色/i,"橙色"],[/\bbrown\b|marrom|marrón|nâu|น้ำตาล|棕色/i,"棕色"],[/\bbeige\b|bege|crema|米色/i,"米色"],[/\bgold\b|dourado|dourada|dorado|dourada|ทอง|金色/i,"金色"],[/\bsilver\b|prateado|prateada|plateado|plateada|prata|เงิน|银色/i,"银色"]])if(re.test(text)){a.push(label);break}let size=text.match(/\b(?:XXS|XS|S|M|L|XL|XXL|XXXL|\d{2,3}\s?(?:mm|cm|inch|in))\b/i);if(size)a.push(size[0].toUpperCase().replace(/\s/g,""));let variant=text.match(/\b(?:mini|pro|max|plus|lite|ultra|portable|waterproof)\b/i);if(variant)a.push(variant[0].toLowerCase());return a.join(" / ")}
function extractQty(text){let m=text.match(/\b(\d+)\s*(?:pcs?|pieces?|packs?|sets?|count|ct|unidades?|unids?|piezas?|pçs?|cai|chiec|ชิ้น|ชุด)\b/i)||text.match(/\b(?:pack|set)\s*(?:of\s*)?(\d+)\b/i)||text.match(/(?:^|\s)(\d+)\s*(?:个|件|只|支|套)(?:\s|$)/);return m?m[1]:""}
function categoryKey(value) { return String(value == null ? "" : value).trim().replace(/\s+/g," ").replace(/\s*&\s*/g,"&").toLowerCase(); }
const CATEGORY_ZH_NAMES={"Audio":"影音娱乐","Amplifiers & Mixers":"扩音器 & 混音器","Audio & Video Cables & Converters":"视听器材&转换器","Home Audio & Speakers":"家用音响","Media Players":"多媒体播放器","Microphones":"麦克风","Others":"其他","AV Receivers":"AV接收机","CD, DVD, & Blu-ray Players":"CD、DVD & 蓝光播放机","CD, DVD & Blu-ray Players":"CD、DVD & 蓝光播放机","Home Theater Systems":"家庭影院系统","MP3 & MP4 Players":"MP3 & MP4 播放机","Radio & Cassette Players":"收音机 & 卡带收音机","Speakers":"家用音响","Voice Recorders":"录音机"};
const categoryTranslationCache=new Map(Object.entries(CATEGORY_ZH_NAMES).map(([a,b])=>[categoryKey(a),b]));try{const savedCategoryTranslations=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pool-category-translation-v1")||"{}");for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(savedCategoryTranslations))if(v)categoryTranslationCache.set(k,v)}catch(e){}
function categoryValues(r) {
  return ["level1_global_be_category","level2_global_be_category","level3_global_be_category"].map(alias => String(getRaw(r,[alias]) || "").trim()).filter(Boolean);
}
function categoryLabel(name) {
  const raw = String(name == null ? "" : name).trim();
  if (!raw) return "";
  if (hasChinese(raw)) return raw;
  const known = categoryTranslationCache.get(categoryKey(raw));
  if (known) return known;
  const cached = referenceTranslationCache.get(raw);
  if (cached && hasChinese(cached)) return cached;
  const local = translateTitle(raw);
  return local !== raw && hasChinese(local) ? local : "";
}
function categoryPath(r) {
  const names = categoryValues(r);
  if (!names.length) return "未提供类目";
  const labels = names.map(categoryLabel);
  return labels.every(Boolean) ? labels.join(" > ") : "类目翻译中";
}
function deriveRecord(r){let item=String(getRaw(r,["item name原文","item_name","item_name_original"])||""),model=String(getRaw(r,["Model name原文","model_name"])||""),mix=item+" "+model,itemCn=String(getRaw(r,["item name中文"])||"").trim(),modelCn=String(getRaw(r,["Model name中文"])||"").trim();if(!itemCn||!/[\u3400-\u9fff]/.test(itemCn)||norm(itemCn)===norm(item))r.autoItemChinese=true;if(!modelCn||!/[\u3400-\u9fff]/.test(modelCn)||norm(modelCn)===norm(model))r.autoModelChinese=true;if(r.autoItemChinese&&!r.localTranslatedItem)r.raw[H[10]]=translateTitle(item);if(r.autoModelChinese&&!r.localTranslatedModel)r.raw[H[12]]=translateTitle(model);let brand=String(getRaw(r,["品牌"])||"").trim(),style=String(getRaw(r,["款式"])||"").trim(),qty=String(getRaw(r,["件数"])||"").trim();if(!brand)r.autoBrand=true;if(!style)r.autoStyle=true;if(!qty)r.autoQty=true;let mallBrand=String(getRaw(r,["mall_brand_name"])||"").trim();if((r.userEditedFields||[]).includes(H[20]))r.autoBrand=false;else{r.autoBrand=true;r.raw[H[20]]=mallBrand||extractBrand(mix)};if(r.autoStyle)r.raw[H[21]]=extractStyle(mix);if(r.autoQty)r.raw[H[22]]=extractQty(mix);invalidateRaw(r)}
function hasChinese(text){return /[\u3400-\u9fff]/.test(String(text||""))}
function translationLanguage(text){
  if(/[\u0e00-\u0e7f]/.test(text))return 'th';
  if(/[ăâđêôơưĂÂĐÊÔƠƯ\u1ea0-\u1ef9]/.test(text))return 'vi';
  if(/[\u0400-\u04ff]/.test(text))return 'ru';
  if(/[\u3040-\u30ff]/.test(text))return 'ja';
  if(/[\uac00-\ud7af]/.test(text))return 'ko';
  if(/[\u0600-\u06ff]/.test(text))return 'ar';
  if(/\b(caixa|som|amplificada|amplificador|microfone|fone|preto|branco|vermelho|prata|roxo|amarelo|cinza|falante|ouvido|cabo|bivolt|sem fio|polegadas|parafuso|tomada|produto|unidade|unidades|rosa|azul|marrom|tamanho)\b/i.test(text))return 'pt';
  if(/\b(pembesar|suara|untuk|warna|hitam|putih|merah|biru|hijau|kuning|kecil|wayar|tanpa|mudah|alih|dengan|bateri|pakej|percuma)\b/i.test(text))return 'ms';
  return 'en';
}
const SIMPLE_CHINESE={black:'黑色',white:'白色',blue:'蓝色',red:'红色',purple:'紫色',pink:'粉色',green:'绿色',yellow:'黄色',brown:'棕色',orange:'橙色',grey:'灰色',gray:'灰色',silver:'银色',gold:'金色','size s':'S号','size m':'M号','size l':'L号',preto:'黑色',branco:'白色',marrom:'棕色',bivolt:'双电压','black and brass':'黑色与黄铜色','น้ำตาล':'棕色','สีน้ําตาล m':'棕色 M号','สีน้ําตาล s':'棕色 S号'};
function translationPassthrough(text){return /^[\u3400-\u9fff\d\s.,，。!！?？:：;；()（）【】\-]+$/.test(text)||/^(?=.*\d)[A-Z0-9+().,*/#-]{1,40}$/.test(text)||['USB','USB-C','AUX','RCA','HDMI','XLR','FM','AM','AC','DC'].includes(text)}
function scheduleTranslationStatus(){clearTimeout(translationStatusTimer);translationStatusTimer=setTimeout(updateTranslationStatus,150)}
function updateTranslationStatus(){const el=$("translationState");if(!el)return;const done=rows.filter(r=>{const item=String(getRaw(r,["item name原文","item_name","item_name_original"])||"").trim(),model=String(getRaw(r,["Model name原文","model_name"])||"").trim();return (!item||translationCache.has(item)||hasChinese(value(r,H[10])))&&(!model||translationCache.has(model)||hasChinese(value(r,H[12])))}).length;el.textContent="参考 HTML 翻译：中文已就绪 "+done.toLocaleString()+" / "+rows.length.toLocaleString()+" 款"+(translationRunning?" · 正在翻译当前页":translationLastError?" · "+translationLastError:"") }
async function requestChineseBatch(texts,source,target='zh'){
  const joined=texts.map((text,i)=>'ZXQ'+String(i).padStart(4,'0')+'QXZ '+text.replace(/[\r\n]+/g,' ')).join('\n');
  const payload={header:{fn:'auto_translation',client_key:'browser-firefox-110.0.0-Windows 10-'+crypto.randomUUID()+'-'+Date.now()},type:'plain',model_category:'normal',source:{lang:source,text_list:['',joined,'']},target:{lang:target}};
  const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),20000);
  let response,data;
  try{response=await fetch('https://transmart.qq.com/api/imt',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(payload),signal:controller.signal,credentials:'omit'});if(!response.ok){const error=new Error(response.status===429?'翻译服务暂时忙，请稍后重试。':'翻译服务连接失败，请稍后重试。');error.pause=true;throw error}data=await response.json()}catch(error){if(error.name==='TypeError'||error.name==='AbortError')error.pause=true;throw error}finally{clearTimeout(timer)}
  if(data?.header?.ret_code!=='succ')throw new Error(data?.message?.includes('engine')?'该语种的翻译通道暂不可用。':'翻译服务暂时忙，请稍后重试。');
  const raw=(data.auto_translation||[]).join(''),markers=[...raw.matchAll(/ZXQ\s*(\d{4})\s*QXZ/gi)];
  if(markers.length!==texts.length||markers.some((m,i)=>Number(m[1])!==i))throw new Error('翻译结果未完整返回，请重试。');
  const result=markers.map((m,i)=>raw.slice(m.index+m[0].length,markers[i+1]?.index??raw.length).trim());
  if(result.some(x=>!x))throw new Error('翻译结果未完整返回，请重试。');
  if(target==='zh'&&result.some((x,i)=>!/[\u3400-\u9fff]/.test(x)&&!translationPassthrough(texts[i])))throw new Error('翻译服务未返回中文，请重试。');
  return result;
}
async function translateQueuedBatch(batch){
  const language=translationLanguage(batch[0].text),texts=batch.map(x=>x.text);
  try{
    const result=['th','ms'].includes(language)?await requestChineseBatch(await requestChineseBatch(texts,language,'en'),'en'):await requestChineseBatch(texts,language);
    return result;
  }catch(error){
    // Smaller requests also keep failures isolated to the affected title/specification.
    if(error.pause)throw error;
    if(batch.length>1){const middle=Math.ceil(batch.length/2);return [...await translateQueuedBatch(batch.slice(0,middle)),...await translateQueuedBatch(batch.slice(middle))]}
    translationLastError='个别商品中文暂未补齐，可稍后重试。已保存译文不受影响。';return [''];
  }
}
function finishTranslation(job,value){if(value)translationCache.set(job.text,value);translationPending.delete(job.text);job.resolve(value||'')}
async function drainTranslations(){
  if(translationRunning)return;translationRunning=true;translationLastError='';scheduleTranslationStatus();
  try{
    while(translationQueue.length){
      if(Date.now()<translationPausedUntil)break;
      const first=translationQueue.shift(),batch=[first],lang=translationLanguage(first.text);let size=first.text.length;
      for(let i=0;i<translationQueue.length&&batch.length<3;){const job=translationQueue[i];if(translationLanguage(job.text)===lang&&size+job.text.length<=300){batch.push(job);size+=job.text.length;translationQueue.splice(i,1)}else i++}
      try{const result=await translateQueuedBatch(batch);batch.forEach((job,i)=>finishTranslation(job,result[i]));saveTranslationCache()}
      catch(error){translationLastError=error.name==='AbortError'?'翻译连接超时，请重试。':error.message;translationPausedUntil=Date.now()+30000;batch.forEach(job=>finishTranslation(job,''));break}
      scheduleTranslationStatus();
      if(translationQueue.length)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,350));
    }
  }finally{while(translationQueue.length)finishTranslation(translationQueue.shift(),'');translationRunning=false;scheduleTranslationStatus()}
}
function translateCn(value){
  const text=String(value||'').trim();if(!text)return Promise.resolve('');
  if(translationCache.has(text))return Promise.resolve(translationCache.get(text));
  const simple=SIMPLE_CHINESE[text.toLowerCase()]||(translationPassthrough(text)?text:'');
  if(simple){translationCache.set(text,simple);saveTranslationCache();return Promise.resolve(simple)}
  if(translationPending.has(text))return translationPending.get(text);
  if(Date.now()<translationPausedUntil)return Promise.resolve('');
  const promise=new Promise(resolve=>translationQueue.push({text,resolve}));translationPending.set(text,promise);
  clearTimeout(translationFlushTimer);translationFlushTimer=setTimeout(drainTranslations,50);
  return promise;
}
async function translateVisible(list){

function mergeBuyerEdits(incoming,previous){let byId=new Map();for(let old of previous||[]){let id=idFor(old);if(id&&!byId.has(id))byId.set(id,old)}for(let r of incoming){let old=byId.get(idFor(r));if(!old)continue;let fields=old.userEditedFields||[];for(let h of fields)if(old.raw&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(old.raw,h))r.raw[h]=old.raw[h];r.userEditedFields=[...new Set(fields)];if(old.preTouched){r.preTouched=true;r.preValue=old.preValue||"";r.preDate=old.preDate||"";r.preNote=old.preNote||""}if(old.manualGroup)r.manualGroup=old.manualGroup;let sameItem=norm(getRaw(old,["item name原文","item_name","item_name_original"]))===norm(getRaw(r,["item name原文","item_name","item_name_original"])),sameModel=norm(getRaw(old,["Model name原文","model_name"]))===norm(getRaw(r,["Model name原文","model_name"]));if(sameItem&&old.localTranslatedItem){r.raw[H[10]]=old.raw[H[10]];r.autoItemChinese=true;r.localTranslatedItem=true}if(sameModel&&old.localTranslatedModel){r.raw[H[12]]=old.raw[H[12]];r.autoModelChinese=true;r.localTranslatedModel=true}if(fields.includes(H[10]))r.autoItemChinese=false;if(fields.includes(H[12]))r.autoModelChinese=false;if(fields.includes(H[20]))r.autoBrand=false;if(fields.includes(H[21]))r.autoStyle=false;if(fields.includes(H[22]))r.autoQty=false;invalidateRaw(r)}return incoming}
async function runDeferredProcessing(token){try{for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++){if(token!==activeHashRun)return;deriveRecord(rows[i]);if(i&&i%400===0){$("state").textContent=(sourceFileName?sourceFileName+" · ":"")+"后台整理字段 "+i+" / "+rows.length;await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0))}}if(token!==activeHashRun)return;for(let r of rows)r.group=r.manualGroup||"UNGROUPED-"+r.uid;save(true);render();if(token===activeHashRun)$("groupState").textContent="字段已整理 · 默认平铺；点击按阈值分组时再处理图片"}catch(e){console.error(e);if(token===activeHashRun){$("groupState").textContent="后台处理失败，可重新归类";toast("后台图片/字段处理失败："+(e?.message||"请重试"))}}}
async function importFile(f){if(!f)return;if(historyMode)returnToCurrent();let hashToken=++activeHashRun,oldRows=rows,oldHeaders=headers,oldSource=sourceFileName,oldGrouping=groupingEnabled;sourceFileName=f.name;$("state").textContent=f.name+" · 正在读取";try{let x=/\.(csv|txt)$/i.test(f.name)||f.type==="text/csv"?await parseCsv(f,pct=>{$("state").textContent=f.name+" · 正在解析 CSV "+pct+"%"}):await parseXlsx(f);headers=x.headers;rows=mergeBuyerEdits(x.rows,oldRows);groupingEnabled=false;for(let r of rows){r.manualGroup="";r.group="UNGROUPED-"+r.uid}opened.clear();selected.clear();filters={};pageIndex=0;$("groupState").textContent="数据已载入 · 图片特征后台准备中；当前平铺未分组";save(true);render();toast(rows.length?"数据已载入："+rows.length+" 条；先展示平铺列表，图片特征将在后台准备":"未找到商品记录："+f.name+" 只有表头/说明，请选择含商品行的数据文件");setTimeout(()=>runDeferredProcessing(hashToken),30)}catch(e){console.error(e);rows=oldRows;headers=oldHeaders;sourceFileName=oldSource;groupingEnabled=oldGrouping;$("state").textContent=(sourceFileName?sourceFileName+" · ":"")+"导入失败";render();toast("导入失败："+(e?.message||"请确认文件为标准 XLSX 或 CSV"))}}

function download(blob,name){if(!(blob instanceof Blob))throw Error("导出文件生成失败");let u=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement("a");a.href=u;a.download=name;a.style.display="none";document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),5000)}

state=$("bulkPreValue").value,date=$("bulkPreDate").value,targets=rows.filter(x=>selected.has(x.uid));if(!targets.length){toast("请先勾选 Model ID");return}if(!state&&!date){toast("请选择 Y/N $("onlineTranslate").onclick=retryVisibleTranslation;$("thresholdPreset").onchange=()=>{if($("thresholdPreset").value!=="custom")$("thresholdInput").value=$("thresholdPreset").value};$("rebuildGroups").onclick=async()=>{if(historyMode)return;threshold=Math.max(1,Math.min(100,Number($("thresholdInput").value)||90));$("thresholdInput").value=threshold;$("thresholdPreset").value=[10,30,50,70,90].includes(threshold)?String(threshold):"custom";groupingEnabled=true;let async function start(){refreshBuyerDatalists();headers=H.slice();rows=[];sourceFileName="";$("import").disabled=true;render();$("state").textContent="正在恢复本机工作台…";let saved=null,fromDB=false;try{let data=await loadWorkbenchState();if(data.meta){saved={rows:data.rows,headers:data.meta.headers||H,sourceFileName:data.meta.sourceFileName||"",threshold:data.meta.threshold,meta:data.meta};fromDB=true}}catch(e){console.error("IndexedDB读取失败",e)}if(!saved){try{saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("pool-workbench-v2")||"null")}catch(e){console.warn("旧版本地数据读取失败",e)}}if(saved?.rows){rows=saved.rows;headers=saved.headers||H;sourceFileName=saved.sourceFileName||"";threshold=Number(saved.threshold)||90;groupingEnabled=!!(saved.meta?.groupingEnabled??saved.groupingEnabled);for(let r of rows)r.group=groupingEnabled?(r.manualGroup||r.group||"UNGROUPED-"+r.uid):"UNGROUPED-"+r.uid;$("thresholdInput").value=threshold;$("thresholdPreset").value=[10,30,50,70,90].includes(threshold)?String(threshold):"custom";render();for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++){deriveRecord(rows[i]);if(i&&i%400===0)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0))}if(!groupingEnabled)for(let r of rows)r.group="UNGROUPED-"+r.uid;if(!fromDB)save(true);else $("saveState").textContent="本机数据已恢复";render()}else{headers=H.slice();rows=[];sourceFileName="";render();$("state").textContent="等待导入商品数据"}try{await refreshVersions()}catch(e){console.warn("历史版本读取失败",e)}$("import").disabled=false}
start().catch(e=>{console.error(e);$("import").disabled=false;$("saveState").textContent="本机数据读取失败";toast("恢复本机工作台失败："+(e?.message||"请刷新页面"))});})();</script></body></html>




















<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>寻品产品分享</title>
<style>*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;padding:24px;background:#f2f6f3;color:#203a2e;font:16px/1.55 "Segoe UI","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif}.page{max-width:1180px;margin:auto}.head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:0 0 12px}.head h1{font-size:24px;margin:0}.head p{margin:2px 0;color:#607269;font-size:12px}.head button{border:1px solid #cbded2;border-radius:8px;background:#fff;padding:8px 12px;color:#285844;font-weight:650}.product{position:relative;background:#fff;border:1px solid #dce7df;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;margin:0 0 16px;break-inside:avoid;box-shadow:0 3px 12px #1835220c}.product-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;align-items:center;border-bottom:1px solid #e7eee9;padding-bottom:7px;margin-bottom:8px;color:#607269}.product-head b{color:#1b3337;white-space:nowrap}.images{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:9px}.images figure{position:relative;margin:0;min-width:0}.images img,.empty{width:100%;height:clamp(180px,32vh,300px);object-fit:contain;background:#f6f8f6;border-radius:9px}.empty{display:grid;place-items:center;color:#89978e}.images figcaption{font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#587064;margin:4px 2px}.share-badges{position:absolute;right:7px;top:7px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-end;gap:4px;max-width:calc(100% - 14px);pointer-events:none}.share-badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;max-width:100%;padding:3px 7px;border:1px solid #ffffffb8;border-radius:999px;background:#fffef0ef;color:#6b4b14;box-shadow:0 2px 8px #18241124;font-size:10px;line-height:1.25;font-weight:750;white-space:nowrap}.share-badge i{font-style:normal;font-size:12px}.share-badge.crown{background:#fff7dd;color:#80550a}.field{display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px minmax(0,1fr);gap:10px;padding:6px 2px;border-bottom:1px solid #edf1ee;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.field b{color:#718078;font-weight:600}.field a{color:#167653;overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-all}.two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}.emptyState{padding:35px;text-align:center;color:#687a71}.footer{margin:8px 0;color:#77867d;font-size:10px;text-align:center}@media(max-width:560px){body{padding:8px}.product{padding:10px}.images img,.empty{height:130px}.field{grid-template-columns:94px 1fr}.head h1{font-size:18px}}@media print{body{background:#fff;padding:0}.product{box-shadow:none;margin-bottom:8px}.head button{display:none}}</style></head>
<body><main class="page"><header class="head"><div><h1>寻品产品信息</h1><p id="summary">正在读取分享内容…</p></div><button type="button" onclick="window.print()">打印 / 保存 PDF</button></header><div id="cards"></div><div class="footer">产品信息来自买手工作看板</div></main>
<script>
function e(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return c==="&"?"&amp;":c==="<"?"&lt;":c===">"?"&gt;":"&quot;"})}
function safeUrl(v){try{var u=new URL(String(v||""));return /^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)?u.href:""}catch(x){return ""}}
function imageUrl(v){var s=String(v||"");return /^data:image\/(?:png|jpe?g|webp|gif);base64,/i.test(s)?s:safeUrl(s)}
function img(src,label){var u=imageUrl(src);return u?("<img src=\""+e(u)+"\" alt=\""+e(label)+"\">"):"<div class=\"empty\">暂无图片</div>"}
function field(name,value){return "<div class=\"field\"><b>"+e(name)+"</b><span>"+e(value||"—")+"</span></div>"}
function badges(items){return "<div class=\"share-badges\">"+(items||[]).map(function(x){return "<span class=\"share-badge "+e(x.kind)+"\"><i>"+e(x.icon)+"</i>"+e(x.text)+"</span>"}).join("")+"</div>"}
function card(p,i){var shopee=safeUrl(p.shopeeUrl);var link=p.shopee?(shopee?"<a href=\""+e(shopee)+"\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">"+e(p.shopee)+"</a>":e(p.shopee)):"—";return "<article class=\"product\"><div class=\"product-head\"><span>产品 "+(i+1)+"</span><b>Model ID "+e(p.id)+"</b></div><div class=\"images\"><figure>"+img(p.main,"主图图片")+badges(p.badges)+"<figcaption>主图图片</figcaption></figure><figure>"+img(p.model,"model 图片（SKU图）")+"<figcaption>model 图片（SKU图）</figcaption></figure></div>"+field("item name 中文",p.itemChinese)+field("Model name 中文",p.modelChinese)+field("上新类目",p.category)+(p.invitation?field("邀请ID",p.invitation):"")+"<div class=\"two\">"+field("目标价",p.target)+field("Model ID",p.id)+"</div><div class=\"field\"><b>Shopee link</b><span>"+link+"</span></div></article>"}
async function load(){var raw=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("v")||(location.hash.indexOf("#d=")===0?location.hash.slice(3):"");if(!raw)throw new Error("分享链接缺少产品数据");var mode=raw.charAt(0),encoded=raw.slice(1).replace(/-/g,"+").replace(/_/g,"/");while(encoded.length%4)encoded+="=";var bytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(encoded),function(c){return c.charCodeAt(0)});var data;if(mode==="z"){if(!("DecompressionStream" in window))throw new Error("当前浏览器不支持解压分享数据");data=await new Response(new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream("gzip"))).json()}else data=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));var products=Array.isArray(data.products)?data.products:[];if(!products.length)throw new Error("分享链接中没有产品");document.title="寻品产品分享 · "+products.length+" 个产品";document.getElementById("summary").textContent=products.length+" 个产品 · 中文标题与类目 · 可截图或打印";document.getElementById("cards").innerHTML=products.map(card).join("")}
load().catch(function(err){document.getElementById("summary").textContent="无法显示分享内容";document.getElementById("cards").innerHTML="<div class=\"emptyState\">"+e(err&&err.message?err.message:"分享链接无效")+"</div>"})
</script></body></html>
